# Plan: LLM-Generated Trail Titles & Descriptions (Agent-Aware)

## Context

Parts A (checkpoints.json) and B (prompt-based titles) are already implemented and passing CI. However, `titleFromPrompt()` just truncates the first line of the user's prompt, producing mediocre titles. The user wants an LLM to generate proper trail titles and descriptions from the session transcript.

This happens **at first condensation** (PostCommit) because:
- Rich context available (prompts + transcript + files touched)
- Only once per trail (first commit)
- No concurrency issues (synchronous in hook process)

**Agent-aware**: Not all agents have non-interactive CLI modes. Claude Code has `claude --print`, but Gemini CLI and OpenCode don't. We add an optional `TextGenerator` interface that agents implement when capable, and skip title generation for agents that can't.

## Implementation

### C1. Add `TextGenerator` optional interface to agent package

**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go`

Add alongside other optional interfaces (`TranscriptAnalyzer`, `HookSupport`, etc.):

```go
// TextGenerator is an optional interface for agents whose CLI supports
// non-interactive text generation (e.g., claude --print).
// Used for AI-powered metadata generation (trail titles, summaries).
type TextGenerator interface {
    // GenerateText sends a prompt to the agent's CLI and returns the raw text response.
    // model is a hint (e.g., "haiku", "sonnet"). Implementations may ignore if not applicable.
    GenerateText(ctx context.Context, prompt string, model string) (string, error)
}
```

### C2. Implement `TextGenerator` for Claude Code

**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/generate.go` (new file)

```go
package claudecode

func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GenerateText(ctx context.Context, prompt string, model string) (string, error) {
    claudePath := "claude"
    if model == "" {
        model = "haiku"
    }
    cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, claudePath,
        "--print", "--output-format", "json",
        "--model", model, "--setting-sources", "")
    cmd.Dir = os.TempDir()
    cmd.Env = stripGitEnv(os.Environ())
    cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
    // Execute, parse {"result": "..."} envelope, return result string
}
```

Includes a local `stripGitEnv()` helper (same 7-line function as in `summarize/claude.go` — simple filter of `GIT_*` env vars; not worth extracting to a shared package for this).

### C3. Refactor existing `ClaudeGenerator` to use `TextGenerator`

**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/summarize/claude.go`

Update `ClaudeGenerator.Generate()` to use `TextGenerator` interface when available, preserving the existing `CommandRunner` injection for tests:

```go
func (g *ClaudeGenerator) Generate(ctx context.Context, input Input) (*checkpoint.Summary, error) {
    transcriptText := FormatCondensedTranscript(input)
    prompt := buildSummarizationPrompt(transcriptText)

    // Use TextGenerator if available (allows agent-aware generation)
    if g.TextGen != nil {
        rawResult, err := g.TextGen.GenerateText(ctx, prompt, g.Model)
        // parse rawResult as checkpoint.Summary JSON
    }

    // Fallback to direct CLI invocation (for tests with CommandRunner)
    // ... existing implementation ...
}
```

Actually, this refactor adds complexity for marginal benefit. **Simpler approach**: Leave `ClaudeGenerator` as-is (it works, tests pass). The new trail title code directly uses the `TextGenerator` interface from the agent. The two code paths (summarize `ClaudeGenerator` for checkpoint summaries, `TextGenerator` for trail titles) share the same underlying Claude CLI but through different layers.

**Decision**: Skip this refactor. Keep `ClaudeGenerator` unchanged. Trail title generation uses `TextGenerator` directly.

### C4. New file: `cmd/entire/cli/summarize/trail_title.go`

Trail title/description generation, agent-aware:

```go
package summarize

const trailTitlePromptTemplate = `Analyze this development session transcript and generate a title and description.

<transcript>
%s
</transcript>

Return a JSON object:
{
  "title": "Short imperative title (max 80 chars)",
  "description": "1-3 sentence description of what was accomplished and why"
}

Guidelines:
- Title: imperative mood, captures core intent (e.g. "Add user authentication flow")
- Description: explain the "what" and "why", not the "how"
- Return ONLY the JSON object`

const trailTitleModel = "haiku"

type TrailTitleResult struct {
    Title       string `json:"title"`
    Description string `json:"description"`
}

func GenerateTrailTitle(ctx context.Context, transcriptBytes []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType) (*TrailTitleResult, error) {
    // 1. Get the active agent and check if it implements TextGenerator
    ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent not found: %w", err)
    }
    gen, ok := ag.(agent.TextGenerator)
    if !ok {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support text generation", agentType)
    }

    // 2. Build condensed transcript (reuse existing infrastructure)
    condensed, err := BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes(transcriptBytes, agentType)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    input := Input{Transcript: condensed, FilesTouched: filesTouched}
    transcriptText := FormatCondensedTranscript(input)

    // 3. Build prompt and call agent's TextGenerator
    prompt := fmt.Sprintf(trailTitlePromptTemplate, transcriptText)
    rawResult, err := gen.GenerateText(ctx, prompt, trailTitleModel)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }

    // 4. Parse JSON response
    cleaned := extractJSONFromMarkdown(rawResult)
    var result TrailTitleResult
    if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(cleaned), &result); err != nil {
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse trail title JSON: %w", err)
    }
    return &result, nil
}
```

### C6. Add `generateTrailTitleFromTranscript` helper

**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`

```go
func generateTrailTitleFromTranscript(repo *git.Repository, branchName string, transcript []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType) {
    if !settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() {
        return
    }
    store := trail.NewStore(repo)
    existing, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)
    if err != nil || existing == nil {
        return
    }
    // Only generate once: skip if description already set
    if existing.Description != "" {
        return
    }
    logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "trail-title")
    result, err := summarize.GenerateTrailTitle(logCtx, transcript, filesTouched, agentType)
    if err != nil {
        logging.Debug(logCtx, "trail title generation skipped",
            slog.String("error", err.Error()))
        return
    }
    //nolint:errcheck // best-effort: trail title generation is non-critical
    store.Update(existing.TrailID, func(m *trail.Metadata) {
        if result.Title != "" {
            m.Title = result.Title
        }
        if result.Description != "" {
            m.Description = result.Description
        }
    })
}
```

branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
if branchName != "" && branchName != GetDefaultBranchName(repo) {
    generateTrailTitleFromTranscript(repo, branchName, result.Transcript, result.FilesTouched, state.AgentType)
}

### C8. Wire into `SaveStep` (auto-commit)

**File**: `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go`

In `SaveStep()`, after the existing `appendCheckpointToTrail()` call (~line 166):

```go
// Generate trail title/description (best-effort, first condensation only)
branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
if branchName != "" && branchName != GetDefaultBranchName(repo) && ctx.TranscriptPath != "" {
    if transcriptData, readErr := os.ReadFile(ctx.TranscriptPath); readErr == nil {
        filesTouched := mergeFilesTouched(nil, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)
        generateTrailTitleFromTranscript(repo, branchName, transcriptData, filesTouched, ctx.AgentType)
    }
}
```

## Design Decisions

1. **`TextGenerator` optional interface** — follows existing pattern (like `TranscriptAnalyzer`, `HookSupport`). Agents opt in when their CLI supports non-interactive generation. Claude Code implements it today; Gemini/OpenCode can add it later.
2. **Haiku model** — trail titles are simple; haiku is fast (~1-2s) and cheap
3. **One-time gate** — `Description != ""` ensures LLM is only called on first condensation per trail
4. **Settings gate** — reuses `settings.IsSummarizeEnabled()`; no new setting
5. **Graceful skip** — if agent doesn't implement `TextGenerator`, title stays as prompt-derived; no error shown to user
6. **No `ClaudeGenerator` refactor** — existing summarize code works; trail titles use `TextGenerator` directly

## Verification

```bash
mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci
```

Manual test:
1. Create a branch, enable entire with `summarize.enabled: true`
2. Run a Claude Code session, make a commit
3. Check `entire trail` output — should show LLM-generated title and description
4. Make a second commit — title should NOT be regenerated (description already set)

    10→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    11→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its
    12→// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.
    13→//
    14→// The interface is organized into four groups:

    16→//   - Identity (5 methods): Name, Type, Description, DetectPresence, ProtectedDirs
    17→//   - Event Mapping (2 methods): HookNames, ParseHookEvent

    22→

    29→

    38→
    39→	// ProtectedDirs returns repo-root-relative directories that should never be

    41→	// Examples: [".claude"] for Claude, [".gemini"] for Gemini.
    42→	ProtectedDirs() []string
    43→

    46→	// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.

    49→	HookNames() []string

    52→	// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).

    52→// DetectPresence checks if Claude Code is configured in the repository.
    53→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    54→	// Get repo root to check for .claude directory
    55→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    56→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    59→		repoRoot = "."
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	// Check for .claude directory
    63→	claudeDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude")
    64→	if _, err := os.Stat(claudeDir); err == nil {
    65→		return true, nil
    66→	}
    67→	// Check for .claude/settings.json
    68→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", "settings.json")
    69→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    70→		return true, nil
    71→	}
    72→	return false, nil
    73→}

    89→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts.
    90→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {

    92→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {

   105→// ReadSession reads a session from Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   106→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSONL bytes.
   107→// ModifiedFiles is computed by parsing the transcript.
   108→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   109→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   110→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	// Read the raw JSONL file
   114→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   120→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   121→	if err != nil {
   122→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   126→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   127→		AgentName:     c.Name(),
   128→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   129→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   130→		NativeData:    data,
   131→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(lines),
   132→	}, nil
   133→}
   134→
   135→// WriteSession writes a session to Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   136→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSONL bytes.
   137→// The session must have been created by Claude Code (AgentName check).
   138→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   139→	if session == nil {
   140→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	// Verify this session belongs to Claude Code
   144→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {
   145→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, c.Name())
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   149→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   153→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   154→	}
   155→
   156→	// Write the raw JSONL data
   157→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   158→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   159→	}
   160→
   161→	return nil
   162→}
   163→
   164→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Claude Code session.
   165→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   166→	return "claude -r " + sessionID
   167→}
   168→
   169→// Session helper methods - work on AgentSession with Claude's native JSONL data
   170→
   171→// GetLastUserPrompt extracts the last user prompt from the session.
   172→// Requires NativeData to be populated (call ReadSession first).
   173→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetLastUserPrompt(session *agent.AgentSession) string {
   174→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   175→		return ""
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   179→	if err != nil {
   180→		return ""
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	return ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines)
   184→}
   185→
   186→// TruncateAtUUID returns a new session truncated at the given UUID (inclusive).
   187→// This is used for rewind operations to restore transcript state.
   188→// Requires NativeData to be populated.
   189→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) TruncateAtUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, uuid string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   190→	if session == nil {
   191→		return nil, errors.New("session is nil")
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   195→		return nil, errors.New("session has no native data")
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	if uuid == "" {
   199→		// No truncation needed, return copy
   200→		return &agent.AgentSession{
   201→			SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   202→			AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   203→			RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   204→			SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   205→			StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   206→			NativeData:    session.NativeData,
   207→			ModifiedFiles: session.ModifiedFiles,
   208→		}, nil
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	// Parse, truncate, re-serialize
   212→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   213→	if err != nil {
   214→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, uuid)
   218→
   219→	newData, err := SerializeTranscript(truncated)
   220→	if err != nil {
   221→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to serialize truncated transcript: %w", err)
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   225→		SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   226→		AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   227→		RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   228→		SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   229→		StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   230→		NativeData:    newData,
   231→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(truncated),
   232→	}, nil
   233→}
   234→
   235→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   236→// for the given tool use ID. Used for task checkpoint rewind.
   237→// Returns the UUID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   238→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FindCheckpointUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   239→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   240→		return "", false
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   244→	if err != nil {
   245→		return "", false
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	return FindCheckpointUUID(lines, toolUseID)
   249→}
   250→
   251→// ReadSessionFromPath is a convenience method that reads a session directly from a file path.
   252→// This is useful when you have the path but not a HookInput.
   253→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSessionFromPath(transcriptPath, sessionID string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   254→	return c.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   255→		SessionID:  sessionID,
   256→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   257→	})
   258→}
   259→
   260→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   261→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   262→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   263→
   264→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   265→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   266→}
   267→
   268→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   269→
   270→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of a Claude Code transcript.
   271→// Claude Code uses JSONL format, so position is the number of lines.
   272→// This is a lightweight operation that only counts lines without parsing JSON.
   273→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   274→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   275→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   276→	if path == "" {
   277→		return 0, nil
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   281→	if err != nil {
   282→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   283→			return 0, nil
   284→		}
   285→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", err)
   286→	}
   287→	defer file.Close()
   288→
   289→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   290→	lineCount := 0
   291→
   292→	for {
   293→		line, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   294→		if err != nil {
   295→			if err == io.EOF {
   296→				if len(line) > 0 {
   297→					lineCount++ // Count final line without trailing newline
   298→				}
   299→				break
   300→			}
   301→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   302→		}
   303→		lineCount++
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	return lineCount, nil
   307→}
   308→
   309→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line number.
   310→// For Claude Code (JSONL format), offset is the starting line number.
   311→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   312→// Returns:
   313→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Claude (from Write/Edit tools)
   314→//   - currentPosition: total number of lines in the file
   315→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   316→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   317→	if path == "" {
   318→		return nil, 0, nil
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	file, openErr := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   322→	if openErr != nil {
   323→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", openErr)
   324→	}
   325→	defer file.Close()
   326→
   327→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   328→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   329→	lineNum := 0
   330→
   331→	for {
   332→		lineData, readErr := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   333→		if readErr != nil && readErr != io.EOF {
   334→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   335→		}
   336→
   337→		if len(lineData) > 0 {
   338→			lineNum++
   339→			if lineNum > startOffset {
   340→				var line TranscriptLine
   341→				if parseErr := json.Unmarshal(lineData, &line); parseErr == nil {
   342→					lines = append(lines, line)
   343→				}
   344→				// Skip malformed lines silently
   345→			}
   346→		}
   347→
   348→		if readErr == io.EOF {
   349→			break
   350→		}
   351→	}
   352→
   353→	return ExtractModifiedFiles(lines), lineNum, nil
   354→}
   355→
   356→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript at line boundaries.
   357→// Claude Code uses JSONL format (one JSON object per line), so chunking
   358→// is done at newline boundaries to preserve message integrity.
   359→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   360→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   361→	if err != nil {
   362→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL transcript: %w", err)
   363→	}
   364→	return chunks, nil
   365→}
   366→
   367→// ReassembleTranscript concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.
   368→//
   369→
   370→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   371→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   372→}
   373→
   374→// SubagentAwareExtractor interface implementation
   375→
   376→// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and any spawned subagents.
   377→// Claude Code spawns subagents via the Task tool; their transcripts are stored in subagentsDir.
   378→// Returns a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   379→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
   380→	return ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)
   381→}
   382→
   383→// CalculateTotalTokenUsage computes token usage including all spawned subagents.
   384→// Claude Code spawns subagents via the Task tool; their transcripts are stored in subagentsDir.
   385→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   386→	return CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)
   387→}

     1→package summarize
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bytes"
     5→	"context"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"errors"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"os"
    10→	"os/exec"
    11→	"strings"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// summarizationPromptTemplate is the prompt used to generate summaries via the Claude CLI.
    17→//
    18→// Security note: The transcript is wrapped in <transcript> tags to provide clear boundary
    19→// markers. This helps contain any potentially malicious content within the transcript
    20→// (e.g., prompt injection attempts in user messages or file contents) by giving the LLM
    21→// a clear structural signal about where the untrusted content begins and ends.
    22→const summarizationPromptTemplate = `Analyze this development session transcript and generate a structured summary.
    23→
    24→<transcript>
    25→%s
    26→</transcript>
    27→
    28→Return a JSON object with this exact structure:
    29→{
    30→  "intent": "What the user was trying to accomplish (1-2 sentences)",
    31→  "outcome": "What was actually achieved (1-2 sentences)",
    32→  "learnings": {
    33→    "repo": ["Codebase-specific patterns, conventions, or gotchas discovered"],
    34→    "code": [{"path": "file/path.go", "line": 42, "end_line": 56, "finding": "What was learned"}],
    35→    "workflow": ["General development practices or tool usage insights"]
    36→  },
    37→  "friction": ["Problems, blockers, or annoyances encountered"],
    38→  "open_items": ["Tech debt, unfinished work, or things to revisit later"]
    39→}
    40→
    41→Guidelines:
    42→- Be concise but specific
    43→- Include line numbers for code learnings when the transcript references specific lines
    44→- Friction should capture both blockers and minor annoyances
    45→- Open items are things intentionally deferred, not failures
    46→- Empty arrays are fine if a category doesn't apply
    47→- Return ONLY the JSON object, no markdown formatting or explanation`
    48→
    49→// DefaultModel is the default model used for summarization.
    50→// Sonnet provides a good balance of quality and cost, with 1M context window
    51→// to handle long transcripts without truncation.
    52→const DefaultModel = "sonnet"
    53→
    54→// ClaudeGenerator generates summaries using the Claude CLI.
    55→type ClaudeGenerator struct {
    56→	// ClaudePath is the path to the claude CLI executable.
    57→	// If empty, defaults to "claude" (expects it to be in PATH).
    58→	ClaudePath string
    59→
    60→	// Model is the Claude model to use for summarization.
    61→	// If empty, defaults to DefaultModel ("sonnet").
    62→	Model string
    63→
    64→	// CommandRunner allows injection of the command execution for testing.
    65→	// If nil, uses exec.CommandContext directly.
    66→	CommandRunner func(ctx context.Context, name string, args ...string) *exec.Cmd
    67→}
    68→
    69→// claudeCLIResponse represents the JSON response from the Claude CLI.
    70→type claudeCLIResponse struct {
    71→	Result string `json:"result"`
    72→}
    73→
    74→// Generate creates a summary from checkpoint data by calling the Claude CLI.
    75→func (g *ClaudeGenerator) Generate(ctx context.Context, input Input) (*checkpoint.Summary, error) {
    76→	// Format the transcript for the prompt
    77→	transcriptText := FormatCondensedTranscript(input)
    78→
    79→	// Build the prompt
    80→	prompt := buildSummarizationPrompt(transcriptText)
    81→
    82→	// Execute the Claude CLI
    83→	runner := g.CommandRunner
    84→	if runner == nil {
    85→		runner = exec.CommandContext
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	claudePath := g.ClaudePath
    89→	if claudePath == "" {
    90→		claudePath = "claude"
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	model := g.Model
    94→	if model == "" {
    95→		model = DefaultModel
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Use empty --setting-sources to skip all settings (user, project, local).
    99→	// This avoids loading MCP servers, hooks, or other config that could interfere
   100→	// with a simple --print summarization call.
   101→	cmd := runner(ctx, claudePath, "--print", "--output-format", "json", "--model", model, "--setting-sources", "")
   102→
   103→	// Fully isolate the subprocess from the user's git repo (ENT-242).
   104→	// Claude Code performs internal git operations (plugin cache, context gathering)
   105→	// that pollute the worktree index with phantom entries from its plugin cache.
   106→	// We must both change the working directory AND strip GIT_* env vars, because
   107→	// git hooks set GIT_DIR which lets Claude Code find the repo regardless of cwd.
   108→	// This also prevents recursive triggering of Entire's own git hooks.
   109→	cmd.Dir = os.TempDir()
   110→	cmd.Env = stripGitEnv(os.Environ())
   111→
   112→	// Pass prompt via stdin
   113→	cmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)
   114→
   115→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   116→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   117→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   118→
   119→	err := cmd.Run()
   120→	if err != nil {
   121→		// Check if the command was not found
   122→		var execErr *exec.Error
   123→		if errors.As(err, &execErr) {
   124→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found: %w", err)
   125→		}
   126→
   127→		// Check for exit error
   128→		var exitErr *exec.ExitError
   129→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   130→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())
   131→		}
   132→
   133→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to run claude CLI: %w", err)
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	// Parse the CLI response
   137→	var cliResponse claudeCLIResponse
   138→	if err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &cliResponse); err != nil {
   139→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse claude CLI response: %w", err)
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	// The result field contains the actual JSON summary
   143→	resultJSON := cliResponse.Result
   144→
   145→	// Try to extract JSON if it's wrapped in markdown code blocks
   146→	resultJSON = extractJSONFromMarkdown(resultJSON)
   147→
   148→	// Parse the summary from the result
   149→	var summary checkpoint.Summary
   150→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(resultJSON), &summary); err != nil {
   151→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse summary JSON: %w (response: %s)", err, resultJSON)
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	return &summary, nil
   155→}
   156→
   157→// buildSummarizationPrompt creates the prompt for the Claude CLI.
   158→func buildSummarizationPrompt(transcriptText string) string {
   159→	return fmt.Sprintf(summarizationPromptTemplate, transcriptText)
   160→}
   161→
   162→// stripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.
   163→// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo.
   164→func stripGitEnv(env []string) []string {
   165→	filtered := make([]string, 0, len(env))
   166→	for _, e := range env {
   167→		if !strings.HasPrefix(e, "GIT_") {
   168→			filtered = append(filtered, e)
   169→		}
   170→	}
   171→	return filtered
   172→}
   173→
   174→// extractJSONFromMarkdown attempts to extract JSON from markdown code blocks.
   175→// If the input is not wrapped in code blocks, it returns the input unchanged.
   176→func extractJSONFromMarkdown(s string) string {
   177→	s = strings.TrimSpace(s)
   178→
   179→	// Check for ```json ... ``` blocks
   180→	if strings.HasPrefix(s, "```json") {
   181→		s = strings.TrimPrefix(s, "```json")
   182→		if idx := strings.LastIndex(s, "```"); idx != -1 {
   183→			s = s[:idx]
   184→		}
   185→		return strings.TrimSpace(s)
   186→	}
   187→
   188→	// Check for ``` ... ``` blocks
   189→	if strings.HasPrefix(s, "```") {
   190→		s = strings.TrimPrefix(s, "```")
   191→		if idx := strings.LastIndex(s, "```"); idx != -1 {
   192→			s = s[:idx]
   193→		}
   194→		return strings.TrimSpace(s)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	return s
   198→}
   199→

     1→// Package summarize provides AI-powered summarization of development sessions.
     2→package summarize
     3→
     4→import (
     5→	"context"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"errors"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"strings"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli"
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint"
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    16→)
    17→
    18→// GenerateFromTranscript generates a summary from raw transcript bytes.
    19→// This is the shared implementation used by both explain --generate and auto-summarize.
    20→//
    21→// Parameters:
    22→//   - ctx: context for cancellation
    23→//   - transcriptBytes: raw transcript bytes (JSONL or JSON format depending on agent)
    24→//   - filesTouched: list of files modified during the session
    25→//   - agentType: the agent type to determine transcript format
    26→//   - generator: summary generator to use (if nil, uses default ClaudeGenerator)
    27→//
    28→// Returns nil, error if transcript is empty or cannot be parsed.
    29→func GenerateFromTranscript(ctx context.Context, transcriptBytes []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType, generator Generator) (*checkpoint.Summary, error) {
    30→	if len(transcriptBytes) == 0 {
    31→		return nil, errors.New("empty transcript")
    32→	}
    33→
    34→	// Build condensed transcript for summarization
    35→	condensed, err := BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes(transcriptBytes, agentType)
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    38→	}
    39→	if len(condensed) == 0 {
    40→		return nil, errors.New("transcript has no content to summarize")
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	input := Input{
    44→		Transcript:   condensed,
    45→		FilesTouched: filesTouched,
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	// Use default generator if none provided
    49→	if generator == nil {
    50→		generator = &ClaudeGenerator{}
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	summary, err := generator.Generate(ctx, input)
    54→	if err != nil {
    55→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to generate summary: %w", err)
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	return summary, nil
    59→}
    60→
    61→// Generator generates checkpoint summaries using an LLM.
    62→type Generator interface {
    63→	// Generate creates a summary from checkpoint data.
    64→	// Returns the generated summary or an error if generation fails.
    65→	Generate(ctx context.Context, input Input) (*checkpoint.Summary, error)
    66→}
    67→
    68→// Input contains condensed checkpoint data for summarization.
    69→type Input struct {
    70→	// Transcript is the condensed transcript entries
    71→	Transcript []Entry
    72→
    73→	// FilesTouched are the files modified during the session
    74→	FilesTouched []string
    75→}
    76→
    77→// EntryType represents the type of a transcript entry.
    78→type EntryType string
    79→
    80→const (
    81→	// EntryTypeUser indicates a user prompt entry.
    82→	EntryTypeUser EntryType = "user"
    83→	// EntryTypeAssistant indicates an assistant response entry.
    84→	EntryTypeAssistant EntryType = "assistant"
    85→	// EntryTypeTool indicates a tool call entry.
    86→	EntryTypeTool EntryType = "tool"
    87→)
    88→
    89→// Entry represents one item in the condensed transcript.
    90→type Entry struct {
    91→	// Type is the entry type (user, assistant, tool)
    92→	Type EntryType
    93→
    94→	// Content is the text content for user/assistant entries
    95→	Content string
    96→
    97→	// ToolName is the name of the tool (for tool entries)
    98→	ToolName string
    99→
   100→	// ToolDetail is a description or file path (for tool entries)
   101→	ToolDetail string
   102→}
   103→
   104→// minimalDetailTools lists tools that should show only essential details in summaries.
   105→// These tools often have verbose outputs that don't add value to summarization.
   106→// The detail shown is typically just a path, URL, or identifier rather than full content.
   107→var minimalDetailTools = map[string]bool{
   108→	"Skill":    true, // Show skill name only, not loaded content
   109→	"Read":     true, // Show file path only, not file contents
   110→	"WebFetch": true, // Show URL only, not fetched content
   111→}
   112→
   113→// BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes parses transcript bytes and extracts a condensed view.
   114→// This is a convenience function that combines parsing and condensing.
   115→// The agentType parameter determines which parser to use (Claude/OpenCode JSONL vs Gemini JSON).
   116→func BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes(content []byte, agentType agent.AgentType) ([]Entry, error) {
   117→	switch agentType {
   118→	case agent.AgentTypeGemini:
   119→		return buildCondensedTranscriptFromGemini(content)
   120→	case agent.AgentTypeOpenCode:
   121→		return buildCondensedTranscriptFromOpenCode(content)
   122→	case agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode, agent.AgentTypeUnknown:
   123→		// Claude format - fall through to shared logic below
   124→	}
   125→	// Claude format (JSONL) - handles Claude Code, Unknown, and any future agent types
   126→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(content)
   127→	if err != nil {
   128→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   129→	}
   130→	return BuildCondensedTranscript(lines), nil
   131→}
   132→
   133→// buildCondensedTranscriptFromGemini parses Gemini JSON transcript and extracts a condensed view.
   134→func buildCondensedTranscriptFromGemini(content []byte) ([]Entry, error) {
   135→	geminiTranscript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript(content)
   136→	if err != nil {
   137→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse Gemini transcript: %w", err)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	var entries []Entry
   141→	for _, msg := range geminiTranscript.Messages {
   142→		switch msg.Type {
   143→		case geminicli.MessageTypeUser:
   144→			if msg.Content != "" {
   145→				entries = append(entries, Entry{
   146→					Type:    EntryTypeUser,
   147→					Content: msg.Content,
   148→				})
   149→			}
   150→		case geminicli.MessageTypeGemini:
   151→			// Add assistant content
   152→			if msg.Content != "" {
   153→				entries = append(entries, Entry{
   154→					Type:    EntryTypeAssistant,
   155→					Content: msg.Content,
   156→				})
   157→			}
   158→			// Add tool calls
   159→			for _, tc := range msg.ToolCalls {
   160→				entries = append(entries, Entry{
   161→					Type:       EntryTypeTool,
   162→					ToolName:   tc.Name,
   163→					ToolDetail: extractGenericToolDetail(tc.Args),
   164→				})
   165→			}
   166→		}
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	return entries, nil
   170→}
   171→
   172→// buildCondensedTranscriptFromOpenCode parses OpenCode JSONL transcript and extracts a condensed view.
   173→func buildCondensedTranscriptFromOpenCode(content []byte) ([]Entry, error) {
   174→	messages, err := opencode.ParseMessages(content)
   175→	if err != nil {
   176→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse OpenCode transcript: %w", err)
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	var entries []Entry
   180→	for _, msg := range messages {
   181→		switch msg.Role {
   182→		case "user":
   183→			if msg.Content != "" {
   184→				entries = append(entries, Entry{
   185→					Type:    EntryTypeUser,
   186→					Content: msg.Content,
   187→				})
   188→			}
   189→		case "assistant":
   190→			if msg.Content != "" {
   191→				entries = append(entries, Entry{
   192→					Type:    EntryTypeAssistant,
   193→					Content: msg.Content,
   194→				})
   195→			}
   196→			for _, part := range msg.Parts {
   197→				if part.Type == "tool" && part.State != nil {
   198→					entries = append(entries, Entry{
   199→						Type:       EntryTypeTool,
   200→						ToolName:   part.Tool,
   201→						ToolDetail: extractGenericToolDetail(part.State.Input),
   202→					})
   203→				}
   204→			}
   205→		}
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	return entries, nil
   209→}
   210→
   211→// extractGenericToolDetail extracts an appropriate detail string from a tool's input/args map.
   212→// Checks common fields in order of preference. Used by both OpenCode and Gemini condensation.
   213→func extractGenericToolDetail(input map[string]interface{}) string {
   214→	for _, key := range []string{"description", "command", "file_path", "path", "pattern"} {
   215→		if v, ok := input[key].(string); ok && v != "" {
   216→			return v
   217→		}
   218→	}
   219→	return ""
   220→}
   221→
   222→// BuildCondensedTranscript extracts a condensed view of the transcript.
   223→// It processes user prompts, assistant responses, and tool calls into
   224→// a simplified format suitable for LLM summarization.
   225→func BuildCondensedTranscript(lines []transcript.Line) []Entry {
   226→	var entries []Entry
   227→
   228→	for _, line := range lines {
   229→		switch line.Type {
   230→		case transcript.TypeUser:
   231→			if entry := extractUserEntry(line); entry != nil {
   232→				entries = append(entries, *entry)
   233→			}
   234→		case transcript.TypeAssistant:
   235→			assistantEntries := extractAssistantEntries(line)
   236→			entries = append(entries, assistantEntries...)
   237→		}
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	return entries
   241→}
   242→
   243→// skillContentPrefix identifies user messages that are skill content injections.
   244→// These are injected after a Skill tool call and contain the full skill instructions.
   245→const skillContentPrefix = "Base directory for this skill:"
   246→
   247→// extractUserEntry extracts a user entry from a transcript line.
   248→// Returns nil if the line doesn't contain a valid user prompt or is skill content.
   249→func extractUserEntry(line transcript.Line) *Entry {
   250→	content := transcript.ExtractUserContent(line.Message)
   251→	if content == "" {
   252→		return nil
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	// Skip skill content injections - these are verbose skill instructions
   256→	// injected as user messages after Skill tool invocations in Claude Code.
   257→	// The prefix "Base directory for this skill:" is added by the superpowers
   258→	// plugin when loading skill content. This filtering reduces transcript noise
   259→	// since skill content is documentation, not user intent.
   260→	if strings.HasPrefix(content, skillContentPrefix) {
   261→		return nil
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	return &Entry{
   265→		Type:    EntryTypeUser,
   266→		Content: content,
   267→	}
   268→}
   269→
   270→// extractAssistantEntries extracts assistant and tool entries from a transcript line.
   271→func extractAssistantEntries(line transcript.Line) []Entry {
   272→	var msg transcript.AssistantMessage
   273→	if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   274→		return nil
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	var entries []Entry
   278→
   279→	for _, block := range msg.Content {
   280→		switch block.Type {
   281→		case transcript.ContentTypeText:
   282→			if block.Text != "" {
   283→				entries = append(entries, Entry{
   284→					Type:    EntryTypeAssistant,
   285→					Content: block.Text,
   286→				})
   287→			}
   288→		case transcript.ContentTypeToolUse:
   289→			var input transcript.ToolInput
   290→			_ = json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input) //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort parsing
   291→
   292→			detail := extractToolDetail(block.Name, input)
   293→
   294→			entries = append(entries, Entry{
   295→				Type:       EntryTypeTool,
   296→				ToolName:   block.Name,
   297→				ToolDetail: detail,
   298→			})
   299→		}
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	return entries
   303→}
   304→
   305→// extractToolDetail extracts an appropriate detail string for a tool call.
   306→// For tools in minimalDetailTools, only essential identifiers are shown.
   307→// For other tools, the full detail chain is used.
   308→func extractToolDetail(toolName string, input transcript.ToolInput) string {
   309→	// For minimal detail tools, extract only the essential identifier
   310→	if minimalDetailTools[toolName] {
   311→		switch toolName {
   312→		case "Skill":
   313→			return input.Skill
   314→		case "Read":
   315→			if input.FilePath != "" {
   316→				return input.FilePath
   317→			}
   318→			return input.NotebookPath
   319→		case "WebFetch":
   320→			return input.URL
   321→		}
   322→	}
   323→
   324→	// For other tools, use the full detail chain
   325→	if input.Description != "" {
   326→		return input.Description
   327→	}
   328→	if input.Command != "" {
   329→		return input.Command
   330→	}
   331→	if input.FilePath != "" {
   332→		return input.FilePath
   333→	}
   334→	if input.NotebookPath != "" {
   335→		return input.NotebookPath
   336→	}
   337→	return input.Pattern
   338→}
   339→
   340→// FormatCondensedTranscript formats an Input into a human-readable string for LLM.
   341→// The format is:
   342→//
   343→//	[User] user prompt here
   344→//
   345→//	[Assistant] assistant response here
   346→//
   347→//	[Tool] ToolName: description or file path
   348→func FormatCondensedTranscript(input Input) string {
   349→	var sb strings.Builder
   350→
   351→	for i, entry := range input.Transcript {
   352→		if i > 0 {
   353→			sb.WriteString("\n")
   354→		}
   355→
   356→		switch entry.Type {
   357→		case EntryTypeUser:
   358→			sb.WriteString("[User] ")
   359→			sb.WriteString(entry.Content)
   360→			sb.WriteString("\n")
   361→		case EntryTypeAssistant:
   362→			sb.WriteString("[Assistant] ")
   363→			sb.WriteString(entry.Content)
   364→			sb.WriteString("\n")
   365→		case EntryTypeTool:
   366→			sb.WriteString("[Tool] ")
   367→			sb.WriteString(entry.ToolName)
   368→			if entry.ToolDetail != "" {
   369→				sb.WriteString(": ")
   370→				sb.WriteString(entry.ToolDetail)
   371→			}
   372→			sb.WriteString("\n")
   373→		}
   374→	}
   375→
   376→	if len(input.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   377→		sb.WriteString("\n[Files Modified]\n")
   378→		for _, file := range input.FilesTouched {
   379→			fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "- %s\n", file)
   380→		}
   381→	}
   382→
   383→	return sb.String()
   384→}
   385→

    14→	logsOnlyScanLimit = 50
    15→
    16→	// maxFirstPromptRunes is the maximum rune length for FirstPrompt stored in session state.
    17→	maxFirstPromptRunes = 100

    19→
    20→// truncatePromptForStorage collapses whitespace and truncates a user prompt
    21→// for storage in FirstPrompt fields.
    22→func truncatePromptForStorage(prompt string) string {
    23→	return stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(prompt), maxFirstPromptRunes, "...")
    24→}

    26→// SessionState is an alias for session.State.

    33→// CheckpointInfo represents checkpoint metadata stored on the sessions branch.
    34→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
    35→type CheckpointInfo struct {
    36→	CheckpointID     id.CheckpointID `json:"checkpoint_id"` // 12-hex-char from Entire-Checkpoint trailer, used as directory path
    37→	SessionID        string          `json:"session_id"`
    38→	CreatedAt        time.Time       `json:"created_at"`
    39→	CheckpointsCount int             `json:"checkpoints_count"`
    40→	FilesTouched     []string        `json:"files_touched"`
    41→	Agent            agent.AgentType `json:"agent,omitempty"` // Human-readable agent name (e.g., "Claude Code")
    42→	IsTask           bool            `json:"is_task,omitempty"`
    43→	ToolUseID        string          `json:"tool_use_id,omitempty"`
    44→	SessionCount     int             `json:"session_count,omitempty"` // Number of sessions (1 if omitted)
    45→	SessionIDs       []string        `json:"session_ids,omitempty"`   // All session IDs in this checkpoint
    46→}
    47→
    48→// CondenseResult contains the result of a session condensation operation.

    51→	SessionID            string

    55→	TotalTranscriptLines int      // Total lines in transcript after this condensation

    66→	ExportData          []byte            // Agent-specific export data (e.g., OpenCode SQLite export)
    67→}

     1→package strategy
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"errors"

    24→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    25→
    26→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5"
