    29→)
    30→
    31→// listCheckpoints returns all checkpoints from the metadata branch.
    32→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    33→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listCheckpoints() ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    34→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    35→	if err != nil {
    36→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	committed, err := store.ListCommitted(context.Background())
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list committed checkpoints: %w", err)
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	// Convert from checkpoint.CommittedInfo to strategy.CheckpointInfo
    45→	result := make([]CheckpointInfo, 0, len(committed))
    46→	for _, c := range committed {
    47→		result = append(result, CheckpointInfo{
    48→			CheckpointID:     c.CheckpointID,
    49→			SessionID:        c.SessionID,
    50→			CreatedAt:        c.CreatedAt,
    51→			CheckpointsCount: c.CheckpointsCount,
    52→			FilesTouched:     c.FilesTouched,
    53→			Agent:            c.Agent,
    54→			IsTask:           c.IsTask,
    55→			ToolUseID:        c.ToolUseID,
    56→			SessionCount:     c.SessionCount,
    57→			SessionIDs:       c.SessionIDs,
    58→		})
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	return result, nil
    62→}
    63→
    64→// getCheckpointsForSession returns all checkpoints for a session ID.
    65→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointsForSession(sessionID string) ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    66→	all, err := s.listCheckpoints()
    67→	if err != nil {
    68→		return nil, err
    69→	}
    70→
    71→	var result []CheckpointInfo
    72→	for _, cp := range all {
    73→		if cp.SessionID == sessionID || strings.HasPrefix(cp.SessionID, sessionID) {
    74→			result = append(result, cp)
    75→		}
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	if len(result) == 0 {
    79→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no checkpoints for session: %s", sessionID)
    80→	}
    81→
    82→	return result, nil
    83→}
    84→
    85→// getCheckpointLog returns the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
    86→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ReadCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    87→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointLog(checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
    88→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), checkpointID)
    94→	if err != nil {
    95→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
    96→	}
    97→	if content == nil {
    98→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", checkpointID)
    99→	}
   100→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   101→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	return content.Transcript, nil
   105→}
   106→
   107→// CondenseSession condenses a session's shadow branch to permanent storage.
   108→// checkpointID is the 12-hex-char value from the Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   109→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
   110→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteCommitted for the git operations.
   111→//
   112→// For mid-session commits (no Stop/SaveStep called yet), the shadow branch may not exist.
   113→// In this case, data is extracted from the live transcript instead.
   114→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSession(repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, state *SessionState, committedFiles map[string]struct{}) (*CondenseResult, error) {
   115→	// Get shadow branch (may not exist for mid-session commits)
   116→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   117→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   118→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   119→	hasShadowBranch := err == nil
   120→
   121→	var sessionData *ExtractedSessionData
   122→	if hasShadowBranch {
   123→		// Extract session data from the shadow branch (with live transcript fallback).
   124→		// Use tracked files from session state instead of collecting all files from tree.
   125→		// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini).
   126→		// Pass live transcript path so condensation reads the current file rather than a
   127→		// potentially stale shadow branch copy (SaveStep may have been skipped if the
   128→		// last turn had no code changes).
   129→		// Pass CheckpointTranscriptStart for accurate token calculation (line offset for Claude, message index for Gemini).
   130→		sessionData, err = s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, state.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   131→		if err != nil {
   132→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data: %w", err)
   133→		}
   134→	} else {
   135→		// No shadow branch: mid-session commit before Stop/SaveStep.
   136→		// Extract data directly from live transcript.
   137→		if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   138→			return nil, errors.New("shadow branch not found and no live transcript available")
   139→		}
   140→		sessionData, err = s.extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state)
   141→		if err != nil {
   142→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data from live transcript: %w", err)
   143→		}
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	// For 1:1 checkpoint model: filter files_touched to only include files actually
   147→	// committed in this specific commit. This ensures each checkpoint represents
   148→	// exactly the files in that commit, not all files mentioned in the transcript.
   149→	if len(committedFiles) > 0 {
   150→		// Track if we had files before filtering to distinguish between:
   151→		// - Session had files but none were committed (don't fallback)
   152→		// - Session had no files to begin with (mid-session commit, fallback OK)
   153→		hadFilesBeforeFiltering := len(sessionData.FilesTouched) > 0
   154→
   155→		if hadFilesBeforeFiltering {
   156→			// Filter to intersection of transcript-extracted files and committed files
   157→			filtered := make([]string, 0, len(sessionData.FilesTouched))
   158→			for _, f := range sessionData.FilesTouched {
   159→				if _, ok := committedFiles[f]; ok {
   160→					filtered = append(filtered, f)
   161→				}
   162→			}
   163→			sessionData.FilesTouched = filtered
   164→		}
   165→
   166→		// Only use committedFiles as fallback for genuine mid-session commits where
   167→		// no files were tracked yet (extraction returned empty). Do NOT fallback when
   168→		// the session had files that simply didn't overlap with the commit - that
   169→		// indicates an unrelated session that shouldn't have its files_touched
   170→		// overwritten with unrelated committed files.
   171→		if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) == 0 && !hadFilesBeforeFiltering {
   172→			sessionData.FilesTouched = make([]string, 0, len(committedFiles))
   173→			for f := range committedFiles {
   174→				sessionData.FilesTouched = append(sessionData.FilesTouched, f)
   175→			}
   176→		}
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	// Get checkpoint store
   180→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   181→	if err != nil {
   182→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Get author info
   186→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   187→	// Calculate attribution. When no shadow branch exists (agent committed mid-turn
   188→	// before SaveStep), pass nil ref — the function uses HEAD as the shadow tree
   189→	// since the agent's commit IS HEAD (no user edits between agent work and commit).
   190→	attribution := calculateSessionAttributions(repo, ref, sessionData, state)
   191→	// Get current branch name
   192→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   193→
   194→	// Generate summary if enabled
   195→	var summary *cpkg.Summary
   196→	if settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() && len(sessionData.Transcript) > 0 {
   197→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   198→		summarizeCtx := logging.WithComponent(logCtx, "summarize")
   199→
   200→		// Scope transcript to this checkpoint's portion.
   201→		// For Claude Code (JSONL), CheckpointTranscriptStart is a line offset.
   202→		// For Gemini (JSON), CheckpointTranscriptStart is a message index.
   203→		var scopedTranscript []byte
   204→		if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   205→			scopedTranscript = geminicli.SliceFromMessage(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   206→		} else {
   207→			scopedTranscript = transcript.SliceFromLine(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   208→		}
   209→		if len(scopedTranscript) > 0 {
   210→			var err error
   211→			summary, err = summarize.GenerateFromTranscript(summarizeCtx, scopedTranscript, sessionData.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, nil)
   212→			if err != nil {
   213→				logging.Warn(summarizeCtx, "summary generation failed",
   214→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   215→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   216→				// Continue without summary - non-blocking
   217→			} else {
   218→				logging.Info(summarizeCtx, "summary generated",
   219→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID))
   220→			}
   221→		}
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	// Write checkpoint metadata using the checkpoint store
   225→	if err := store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), cpkg.WriteCommittedOptions{
   226→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   227→		SessionID:                   state.SessionID,
   228→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameManualCommit,
   229→		Branch:                      branchName,
   230→		Transcript:                  sessionData.Transcript,
   231→		Prompts:                     sessionData.Prompts,
   232→		Context:                     sessionData.Context,
   233→		FilesTouched:                sessionData.FilesTouched,
   234→		CheckpointsCount:            state.StepCount,
   235→		EphemeralBranch:             shadowBranchName,
   236→		AuthorName:                  authorName,
   237→		AuthorEmail:                 authorEmail,
   238→		Agent:                       state.AgentType,
   239→		TurnID:                      state.TurnID,
   240→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: state.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart,
   241→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   state.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
   242→		TokenUsage:                  sessionData.TokenUsage,
   243→		ExportData:                  sessionData.ExportData,
   244→		InitialAttribution:          attribution,
   245→		Summary:                     summary,
   246→	}); err != nil {
   247→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to write checkpoint metadata: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	return &CondenseResult{
   251→		CheckpointID:         checkpointID,
   252→		SessionID:            state.SessionID,
   253→		CheckpointsCount:     state.StepCount,
   254→		FilesTouched:         sessionData.FilesTouched,
   255→		Prompts:              sessionData.Prompts,
   256→		TotalTranscriptLines: sessionData.FullTranscriptLines,
   257→	}, nil
   258→}
   259→
   260→func calculateSessionAttributions(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef *plumbing.Reference, sessionData *ExtractedSessionData, state *SessionState) *cpkg.InitialAttribution {
   261→	// Calculate initial attribution using accumulated prompt attribution data.
   262→	// This uses user edits captured at each prompt start (before agent works),
   263→	// plus any user edits after the final checkpoint (shadow → head).
   264→	//
   265→	// When shadowRef is nil (agent committed mid-turn before SaveStep),
   266→	// HEAD is used as the shadow tree. This is correct because the agent's
   267→	// commit IS HEAD — there are no user edits between agent work and commit.
   268→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   269→
   270→	headRef, headErr := repo.Head()
   271→	if headErr != nil {
   272→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD",
   273→			slog.String("error", headErr.Error()))
   274→		return nil
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	headCommit, commitErr := repo.CommitObject(headRef.Hash())
   278→	if commitErr != nil {
   279→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD commit",
   280→			slog.String("error", commitErr.Error()))
   281→		return nil
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	headTree, treeErr := headCommit.Tree()
   285→	if treeErr != nil {
   286→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD tree",
   287→			slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
   288→		return nil
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	// Get shadow tree: from shadow branch if available, otherwise HEAD (agent committed directly).
   292→	var shadowTree *object.Tree
   293→	if shadowRef != nil {
   294→		shadowCommit, shadowErr := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef.Hash())
   295→		if shadowErr != nil {
   296→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow commit",
   297→				slog.String("error", shadowErr.Error()),
   298→				slog.String("shadow_ref", shadowRef.Hash().String()))
   299→			return nil
   300→		}
   301→		var shadowTreeErr error
   302→		shadowTree, shadowTreeErr = shadowCommit.Tree()
   303→		if shadowTreeErr != nil {
   304→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow tree",
   305→				slog.String("error", shadowTreeErr.Error()))
   306→			return nil
   307→		}
   308→	} else {
   309→		// No shadow branch: agent committed mid-turn. Use HEAD as shadow
   310→		// because the agent's work is the commit itself.
   311→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: using HEAD as shadow (no shadow branch)")
   312→		shadowTree = headTree
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	// Get base tree (state before session started)
   316→	var baseTree *object.Tree
   317→	attrBase := state.AttributionBaseCommit
   318→	if attrBase == "" {
   319→		attrBase = state.BaseCommit // backward compat
   320→	}
   321→	if baseCommit, baseErr := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(attrBase)); baseErr == nil {
   322→		if tree, baseTErr := baseCommit.Tree(); baseTErr == nil {
   323→			baseTree = tree
   324→		} else {
   325→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base tree unavailable",
   326→				slog.String("error", baseTErr.Error()))
   327→		}
   328→	} else {
   329→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base commit unavailable",
   330→			slog.String("error", baseErr.Error()),
   331→			slog.String("attribution_base", attrBase))
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	// Log accumulated prompt attributions for debugging
   335→	var totalUserAdded, totalUserRemoved int
   336→	for i, pa := range state.PromptAttributions {
   337→		totalUserAdded += pa.UserLinesAdded
   338→		totalUserRemoved += pa.UserLinesRemoved
   339→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution data",
   340→			slog.Int("checkpoint", pa.CheckpointNumber),
   341→			slog.Int("user_added", pa.UserLinesAdded),
   342→			slog.Int("user_removed", pa.UserLinesRemoved),
   343→			slog.Int("agent_added", pa.AgentLinesAdded),
   344→			slog.Int("agent_removed", pa.AgentLinesRemoved),
   345→			slog.Int("index", i))
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	attribution := CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
   349→		baseTree,
   350→		shadowTree,
   351→		headTree,
   352→		sessionData.FilesTouched,
   353→		state.PromptAttributions,
   354→	)
   355→
   356→	if attribution != nil {
   357→		logging.Info(logCtx, "attribution calculated",
   358→			slog.Int("agent_lines", attribution.AgentLines),
   359→			slog.Int("human_added", attribution.HumanAdded),
   360→			slog.Int("human_modified", attribution.HumanModified),
   361→			slog.Int("human_removed", attribution.HumanRemoved),
   362→			slog.Int("total_committed", attribution.TotalCommitted),
   363→			slog.Float64("agent_percentage", attribution.AgentPercentage),
   364→			slog.Int("accumulated_user_added", totalUserAdded),
   365→			slog.Int("accumulated_user_removed", totalUserRemoved),
   366→			slog.Int("files_touched", len(sessionData.FilesTouched)))
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	return attribution
   370→}
   371→
   372→// extractSessionData extracts session data from the shadow branch.
   373→// filesTouched is the list of files tracked during the session (from SessionState.FilesTouched).
   374→// agentType identifies the agent (e.g., "Gemini CLI", "Claude Code") to determine transcript format.
   375→// liveTranscriptPath, when non-empty and readable, is preferred over the shadow branch copy.
   376→// This handles the case where SaveStep was skipped (no code changes) but the transcript
   377→// continued growing — the shadow branch copy would be stale.
   378→// checkpointTranscriptStart is the line offset (Claude) or message index (Gemini) where the current checkpoint began.
   379→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionData(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef plumbing.Hash, sessionID string, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType, liveTranscriptPath string, checkpointTranscriptStart int) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   380→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef)
   381→	if err != nil {
   382→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   383→	}
   384→
   385→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   386→	if err != nil {
   387→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   391→	// sessionID is already an "entire session ID" (with date prefix)
   392→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   393→
   394→	// Extract transcript — prefer the live file when available, fall back to shadow branch.
   395→	// The shadow branch copy may be stale if the last turn ended without code changes
   396→	// (SaveStep is only called when there are file modifications).
   397→	var fullTranscript string
   398→	if liveTranscriptPath != "" {
   399→		if liveData, readErr := os.ReadFile(liveTranscriptPath); readErr == nil && len(liveData) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session state
   400→			fullTranscript = string(liveData)
   401→		}
   402→	}
   403→	if fullTranscript == "" {
   404→		// Fall back to shadow branch copy
   405→		if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   406→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   407→				fullTranscript = content
   408→			}
   409→		} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   410→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   411→				fullTranscript = content
   412→			}
   413→		}
   414→	}
   415→
   416→	// Process transcript based on agent type
   417→	if fullTranscript != "" {
   418→		data.Transcript = []byte(fullTranscript)
   419→		data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(agentType, fullTranscript)
   420→		data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(agentType, fullTranscript)
   421→		data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   422→	}
   423→
   424→	// Use tracked files from session state (not all files in tree)
   425→	data.FilesTouched = filesTouched
   426→
   427→	// Read export data from local metadata directory (e.g., OpenCode SQLite export).
   428→	// This is written by lifecycle.go during TurnEnd and must be stored in the
   429→	// committed checkpoint so resume/rewind can re-import the session.
   430→	exportRelPath := metadataDir + "/" + paths.ExportDataFileName
   431→	if exportAbsPath, absErr := paths.AbsPath(exportRelPath); absErr == nil {
   432→		if exportBytes, readErr := os.ReadFile(exportAbsPath); readErr == nil && len(exportBytes) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session metadata
   433→			data.ExportData = exportBytes
   434→		}
   435→	}
   436→
   437→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   438→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   439→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(agentType, data.Transcript, checkpointTranscriptStart)
   440→	}
   441→
   442→	return data, nil
   443→}
   444→
   445→// extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript extracts session data directly from the live transcript file.
   446→// This is used for mid-session commits where no shadow branch exists yet.
   447→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   448→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   449→
   450→	// Read the live transcript
   451→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   452→		return nil, errors.New("no transcript path in session state")
   453→	}
   454→
   455→	liveData, err := os.ReadFile(state.TranscriptPath)
   456→	if err != nil {
   457→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read live transcript: %w", err)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	if len(liveData) == 0 {
   461→		return nil, errors.New("live transcript is empty")
   462→	}
   463→
   464→	fullTranscript := string(liveData)
   465→	data.Transcript = liveData
   466→	data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   467→	data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   468→	data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   469→
   470→	// Extract files from transcript since state.FilesTouched may be empty for mid-session commits
   471→	// (no SaveStep/Stop has been called yet to populate it)
   472→	if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   473→		data.FilesTouched = state.FilesTouched
   474→	} else {
   475→		// Use the shared helper which includes subagent transcripts
   476→		data.FilesTouched = s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   477→	}
   478→
   479→	// Read export data from local metadata directory
   480→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(state.SessionID)
   481→	exportRelPath := metadataDir + "/" + paths.ExportDataFileName
   482→	if exportAbsPath, absErr := paths.AbsPath(exportRelPath); absErr == nil {
   483→		if exportBytes, readErr := os.ReadFile(exportAbsPath); readErr == nil && len(exportBytes) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session metadata
   484→			data.ExportData = exportBytes
   485→		}
   486→	}
   487→
   488→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   489→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   490→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(state.AgentType, data.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   491→	}
   492→
   493→	return data, nil
   494→}
   495→
   496→// countTranscriptItems counts lines (JSONL) or messages (JSON) in a transcript.
   497→// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.
   498→// For Gemini CLI and JSON-based agents, this counts messages.
   499→// Returns 0 if the content is empty or malformed.
   500→func countTranscriptItems(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) int {
   501→	if content == "" {
   502→		return 0
   503→	}
   504→
   505→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   506→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   507→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript([]byte(content))
   508→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   509→			return len(transcript.Messages)
   510→		}
   511→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return 0
   512→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   513→			return 0
   514→		}
   515→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   516→	}
   517→
   518→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   519→	allLines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   520→	// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
   521→	for len(allLines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(allLines[len(allLines)-1]) == "" {
   522→		allLines = allLines[:len(allLines)-1]
   523→	}
   524→	return len(allLines)
   525→}
   526→
   527→// extractUserPrompts extracts all user prompts from transcript content.
   528→// Returns prompts with IDE context tags stripped (e.g., <ide_opened_file>).
   529→func extractUserPrompts(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) []string {
   530→	if content == "" {
   531→		return nil
   532→	}
   533→
   534→	// OpenCode uses JSONL with a different per-line schema than Claude Code
   535→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeOpenCode {
   536→		prompts, err := opencode.ExtractAllUserPrompts([]byte(content))
   537→		if err == nil && len(prompts) > 0 {
   538→			cleaned := make([]string, 0, len(prompts))
   539→			for _, prompt := range prompts {
   540→				if stripped := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(prompt); stripped != "" {
   541→					cleaned = append(cleaned, stripped)
   542→				}
   543→			}
   544→			return cleaned
   545→		}
   546→		return nil
   547→	}
   548→
   549→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   550→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   551→		prompts, err := geminicli.ExtractAllUserPrompts([]byte(content))
   552→		if err == nil && len(prompts) > 0 {
   553→			// Strip IDE context tags for consistency with Claude Code handling
   554→			cleaned := make([]string, 0, len(prompts))
   555→			for _, prompt := range prompts {
   556→				if stripped := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(prompt); stripped != "" {
   557→					cleaned = append(cleaned, stripped)
   558→				}
   559→			}
   560→			return cleaned
   561→		}
   562→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return nil
   563→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   564→			return nil
   565→		}
   566→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   567→	}
   568→
   569→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   570→	return extractUserPromptsFromLines(strings.Split(content, "\n"))
   571→}
   572→
   573→// calculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from raw transcript data.
   574→// startOffset is the line number (Claude Code) or message index (Gemini CLI)
   575→// where the current checkpoint began, allowing calculation for only the portion
   576→// of the transcript since the last checkpoint.
   577→func calculateTokenUsage(agentType agent.AgentType, data []byte, startOffset int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   578→	if len(data) == 0 {
   579→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   580→	}
   581→
   582→	// OpenCode uses JSONL with token info on assistant messages (different schema from Claude Code)
   583→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeOpenCode {
   584→		return opencode.CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes(data, startOffset)
   585→	}
   586→
   587→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   588→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   589→		// Attempt to parse as Gemini JSON
   590→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript(data)
   591→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   592→			return geminicli.CalculateTokenUsage(data, startOffset)
   593→		}
   594→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return empty usage
   595→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   596→			return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   597→		}
   598→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   599→	}
   600→
   601→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   602→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   603→	if err != nil || len(lines) == 0 {
   604→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   605→	}
   606→	// Slice transcript lines to only include checkpoint portion
   607→	if startOffset > 0 && startOffset < len(lines) {
   608→		lines = lines[startOffset:]
   609→	}
   610→	return claudecode.CalculateTokenUsage(lines)
   611→}
   612→
   613→// extractUserPromptsFromLines extracts user prompts from JSONL transcript lines.
   614→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the results.
   615→func extractUserPromptsFromLines(lines []string) []string {
   616→	var prompts []string
   617→	for _, line := range lines {
   618→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   619→		if line == "" {
   620→			continue
   621→		}
   622→
   623→		var entry map[string]interface{}
   624→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry); err != nil {
   625→			continue
   626→		}
   627→
   628→		// Check for user message (supports both "human" and "user" types)
   629→		msgType, ok := entry["type"].(string)
   630→		if !ok || (msgType != "human" && msgType != "user") {
   631→			continue
   632→		}
   633→
   634→		// Extract message content
   635→		message, ok := entry["message"].(map[string]interface{})
   636→		if !ok {
   637→			continue
   638→		}
   639→
   640→		// Handle string content
   641→		if content, ok := message["content"].(string); ok && content != "" {
   642→			cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content)
   643→			if cleaned != "" {
   644→				prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   645→			}
   646→			continue
   647→		}
   648→
   649→		// Handle array content (e.g., multiple text blocks from VSCode)
   650→		if arr, ok := message["content"].([]interface{}); ok {
   651→			var texts []string
   652→			for _, item := range arr {
   653→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   654→					if m["type"] == "text" {
   655→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   656→							texts = append(texts, text)
   657→						}
   658→					}
   659→				}
   660→			}
   661→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   662→				cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n"))
   663→				if cleaned != "" {
   664→					prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   665→				}
   666→			}
   667→		}
   668→	}
   669→	return prompts
   670→}
   671→
   672→// generateContextFromPrompts generates context.md content from a list of prompts.
   673→func generateContextFromPrompts(prompts []string) []byte {
   674→	if len(prompts) == 0 {
   675→		return nil
   676→	}
   677→
   678→	var buf strings.Builder
   679→	buf.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   680→	buf.WriteString("## User Prompts\n\n")
   681→
   682→	for i, prompt := range prompts {
   683→		// Truncate very long prompts for readability
   684→		displayPrompt := prompt
   685→		if len(displayPrompt) > 500 {
   686→			displayPrompt = displayPrompt[:500] + "..."
   687→		}
   688→		buf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n", i+1))
   689→		buf.WriteString(displayPrompt)
   690→		buf.WriteString("\n\n")
   691→	}
   692→
   693→	return []byte(buf.String())
   694→}
   695→
   696→// CondenseSessionByID force-condenses a session by its ID and cleans up.
   697→// This is used by "entire doctor" to salvage stuck sessions.
   698→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSessionByID(sessionID string) error {
   699→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "condense-by-id")
   700→
   701→	// Load session state
   702→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   703→	if err != nil {
   704→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   705→	}
   706→	if state == nil {
   707→		return fmt.Errorf("session not found: %s", sessionID)
   708→	}
   709→
   710→	// Open repository
   711→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   712→	if err != nil {
   713→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   714→	}
   715→
   716→	// Generate a checkpoint ID
   717→	checkpointID, err := id.Generate()
   718→	if err != nil {
   719→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   720→	}
   721→
   722→	// Check if shadow branch exists (required for condensation)
   723→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   724→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   725→	_, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   726→	hasShadowBranch := refErr == nil
   727→
   728→	if !hasShadowBranch {
   729→		// No shadow branch means no checkpoint data to condense.
   730→		// Just clean up the state file.
   731→		logging.Info(ctx, "no shadow branch for session, clearing state only",
   732→			slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   733→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   734→		)
   735→		if err := s.clearSessionState(sessionID); err != nil {
   736→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
   737→		}
   738→		return nil
   739→	}
   740→
   741→	// Condense the session
   742→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state, nil)
   743→	if err != nil {
   744→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to condense session: %w", err)
   745→	}
   746→
   747→	logging.Info(ctx, "session condensed by ID",
   748→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   749→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   750→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   751→	)
   752→
   753→	// Update session state: reset step count and transition to idle
   754→	state.StepCount = 0
   755→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   756→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   757→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   758→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = state.BaseCommit
   759→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   760→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   761→
   762→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   763→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   764→	}
   765→
   766→	// Clean up shadow branch if no other sessions need it
   767→	if err := s.cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(repo, shadowBranchName, sessionID); err != nil {
   768→		logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to clean up shadow branch",
   769→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   770→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   771→		)
   772→		// Non-fatal: condensation succeeded, shadow branch cleanup is best-effort
   773→	}
   774→
   775→	return nil
   776→}
   777→
   778→// cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused deletes a shadow branch if no other active sessions reference it.
   779→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(_ *git.Repository, shadowBranchName, excludeSessionID string) error {
   780→	// List all session states to check if any other session uses this shadow branch
   781→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   782→	if err != nil {
   783→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   784→	}
   785→
   786→	for _, state := range allStates {
   787→		if state.SessionID == excludeSessionID {
   788→			continue
   789→		}
   790→		otherShadow := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   791→		if otherShadow == shadowBranchName && state.StepCount > 0 {
   792→			// Another session still needs this shadow branch
   793→			return nil
   794→		}
   795→	}
   796→
   797→	// No other sessions need it, delete the shadow branch via CLI
   798→	// (go-git v5's RemoveReference doesn't persist with packed refs/worktrees)
   799→	if err := DeleteBranchCLI(shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   800→		// Branch already gone is not an error
   801→		if errors.Is(err, ErrBranchNotFound) {
   802→			return nil
   803→		}
   804→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch: %w", err)
   805→	}
   806→	return nil
   807→}
   808→

    33→// hasTTY checks if /dev/tty is available for interactive prompts.
    34→// Returns false when running as an agent subprocess (no controlling terminal).
    35→//
    36→// In test environments, ENTIRE_TEST_TTY overrides the real check:
    37→//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 → simulate human (TTY available)
    38→//   - ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0 → simulate agent (no TTY)
    39→func hasTTY() bool {
    40→	if v := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY"); v != "" {
    41→		return v == "1"
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	// Gemini CLI sets GEMINI_CLI=1 when running shell commands.
    45→	// Gemini subprocesses may have access to the user's TTY, but they can't
    46→	// actually respond to interactive prompts. Treat them as non-TTY.
    47→	// See: https://geminicli.com/docs/tools/shell/
    48→	if os.Getenv("GEMINI_CLI") != "" {
    49→		return false
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		return false
    55→	}
    56→	_ = tty.Close()
    57→	return true
    58→}
    59→
    60→// askConfirmTTY prompts the user for a yes/no confirmation via /dev/tty.
    61→// This works even when stdin is redirected (e.g., git commit -m).
    62→// Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.
    63→// If context is non-empty, it is displayed on a separate line before the prompt.
    64→func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
    65→	// In test mode, don't try to interact with the real TTY — just use the default.
    66→	// ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1 simulates "a human is present" for the hasTTY() check
    67→	// but we can't actually read from the TTY in tests.
    68→	if os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_TTY") != "" {
    69→		return defaultYes
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	// Gemini CLI sets GEMINI_CLI=1 when running shell commands (including git commit).
    73→	// The agent can't respond to TTY prompts, so use the default to avoid hanging.
    74→	// See: https://geminicli.com/docs/tools/shell/
    75→	if os.Getenv("GEMINI_CLI") != "" {
    76→		return defaultYes
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// Open /dev/tty for both reading and writing
    80→	// This is the controlling terminal, which works even when stdin/stderr are redirected
    81→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    82→	if err != nil {
    83→		// Can't open TTY (e.g., running in CI), use default
    84→		return defaultYes
    85→	}
    86→	defer tty.Close()
    87→
    88→	// Show context if provided
    89→	if context != "" {
    90→		fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s\n", context)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	// Show prompt with default indicator
    94→	// Write to tty directly, not stderr, since git hooks may redirect stderr to /dev/null
    95→	var hint string
    96→	if defaultYes {
    97→		hint = "[Y/n]"
    98→	} else {
    99→		hint = "[y/N]"
   100→	}
   101→	fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s %s ", prompt, hint)
   102→
   103→	// Read response
   104→	reader := bufio.NewReader(tty)
   105→	response, err := reader.ReadString('\n')
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		return defaultYes
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	response = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(response))
   111→	switch response {
   112→	case "y", "yes":
   113→		return true
   114→	case "n", "no":
   115→		return false
   116→	default:
   117→		// Empty or invalid input - use default
   118→		return defaultYes
   119→	}
   120→}
   121→
   122→// CommitMsg is called by the git commit-msg hook after the user edits the message.
   123→// If the message contains only our trailer (no actual user content), strip it
   124→// so git will abort the commit due to empty message.
   125→//
   126→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
   127→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CommitMsg(commitMsgFile string) error {
   128→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from git hook
   129→	if err != nil {
   130→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	message := string(content)
   134→
   135→	// Check if our trailer is present (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
   136→	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); !found {
   137→		// No trailer, nothing to do
   138→		return nil
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	// Check if there's any user content (non-comment, non-trailer lines)
   142→	if !hasUserContent(message) {
   143→		// No user content - strip the trailer so git aborts
   144→		message = stripCheckpointTrailer(message)
   145→		if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
   146→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   147→		}
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	return nil
   151→}
   152→
   153→// hasUserContent checks if the message has any content besides comments and our trailer.
   154→func hasUserContent(message string) bool {
   155→	trailerPrefix := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ":"
   156→	for _, line := range strings.Split(message, "\n") {
   157→		trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)
   158→		// Skip empty lines
   159→		if trimmed == "" {
   160→			continue
   161→		}
   162→		// Skip comment lines
   163→		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "#") {
   164→			continue
   165→		}
   166→		// Skip our trailer line
   167→		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, trailerPrefix) {
   168→			continue
   169→		}
   170→		// Found user content
   171→		return true
   172→	}
   173→	return false
   174→}
   175→
   176→// stripCheckpointTrailer removes the Entire-Checkpoint trailer line from the message.
   177→func stripCheckpointTrailer(message string) string {
   178→	trailerPrefix := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ":"
   179→	var result []string
   180→	for _, line := range strings.Split(message, "\n") {
   181→		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(line), trailerPrefix) {
   182→			result = append(result, line)
   183→		}
   184→	}
   185→	return strings.Join(result, "\n")
   186→}
   187→
   188→// isGitSequenceOperation checks if git is currently in the middle of a rebase,
   189→// cherry-pick, or revert operation. During these operations, commits are being
   190→// replayed and should not be linked to agent sessions.
   191→//
   192→// Detects:
   193→//   - rebase: .git/rebase-merge/ or .git/rebase-apply/ directories
   194→//   - cherry-pick: .git/CHERRY_PICK_HEAD file
   195→//   - revert: .git/REVERT_HEAD file
   196→func isGitSequenceOperation() bool {
   197→	// Get git directory (handles worktrees and relative paths correctly)
   198→	gitDir, err := GetGitDir()
   199→	if err != nil {
   200→		return false // Can't determine, assume not in sequence operation
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	// Check for rebase state directories
   204→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-merge")); err == nil {
   205→		return true
   206→	}
   207→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-apply")); err == nil {
   208→		return true
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	// Check for cherry-pick and revert state files
   212→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "CHERRY_PICK_HEAD")); err == nil {
   213→		return true
   214→	}
   215→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "REVERT_HEAD")); err == nil {
   216→		return true
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	return false
   220→}
   221→
   222→// PrepareCommitMsg is called by the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   223→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer to the commit message with a stable checkpoint ID.
   224→// Only adds a trailer if there's actually new session content to condense.
   225→// The actual condensation happens in PostCommit - if the user removes the trailer,
   226→// the commit will not be linked to the session (useful for "manual" commits).
   227→// For amended commits, preserves the existing checkpoint ID.
   228→//
   229→// The source parameter indicates how the commit was initiated:
   230→//   - "" or "template": normal editor flow - adds trailer with explanatory comment
   231→//   - "message": using -m or -F flag - prompts user interactively via /dev/tty
   232→//   - "merge", "squash": skip trailer entirely (auto-generated messages)
   233→//   - "commit": amend operation - preserves existing trailer or restores from LastCheckpointID
   234→//
   235→
   236→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source string) error {
   237→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   238→
   239→	// Skip during rebase, cherry-pick, or revert operations
   240→	// These are replaying existing commits and should not be linked to agent sessions
   241→	if isGitSequenceOperation() {
   242→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped during git sequence operation",
   243→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   244→			slog.String("source", source),
   245→		)
   246→		return nil
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	// Skip for merge and squash sources
   250→	// These are auto-generated messages - not from Claude sessions
   251→	switch source {
   252→	case "merge", "squash":
   253→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped for source",
   254→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   255→			slog.String("source", source),
   256→		)
   257→		return nil
   258→	}
   259→
   260→	// Handle amend (source="commit") separately: preserve or restore trailer
   261→	if source == "commit" {
   262→		return s.handleAmendCommitMsg(logCtx, commitMsgFile)
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   266→	if err != nil {
   267→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   271→	if err != nil {
   272→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   273→	}
   274→
   275→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   276→	// We match by worktree (not BaseCommit) because the user may have made
   277→	// intermediate commits without entering new prompts, causing HEAD to diverge
   278→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   279→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   280→		// No active sessions or error listing - silently skip (hooks must be resilient)
   281→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no active sessions",
   282→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   283→			slog.String("source", source),
   284→		)
   285→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   286→	}
   287→
   288→	// Fast path: when an agent is committing (ACTIVE session + no TTY), skip
   289→	// content detection and interactive prompts. The agent can't respond to TTY
   290→	// prompts and the content detection can miss mid-session work (no shadow
   291→	// branch yet, transcript analysis may fail). Generate a checkpoint ID and
   292→	// add the trailer directly.
   293→	if !hasTTY() {
   294→		for _, state := range sessions {
   295→			if state.Phase.IsActive() {
   296→				return s.addTrailerForAgentCommit(logCtx, commitMsgFile, state, source)
   297→			}
   298→		}
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	// Check if any session has new content to condense
   302→	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
   303→
   304→	if len(sessionsWithContent) == 0 {
   305→		// No new content — no trailer needed
   306→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no content to link",
   307→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   308→			slog.String("source", source),
   309→			slog.Int("sessions_found", len(sessions)),
   310→		)
   311→		return nil
   312→	}
   313→
   314→	// Read current commit message
   315→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   316→	if err != nil {
   317→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	message := string(content)
   321→
   322→	// Check if trailer already exists (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
   323→	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
   324→		// Trailer already exists (e.g., amend) - keep it
   325→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer already exists",
   326→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   327→			slog.String("source", source),
   328→			slog.String("existing_checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
   329→		)
   330→		return nil
   331→	}
   332→
   333→	// Generate a fresh checkpoint ID
   334→	checkpointID, err := id.Generate()
   335→	if err != nil {
   336→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	// Determine agent type and last prompt from session
   340→	agentType := DefaultAgentType // default for backward compatibility
   341→	var lastPrompt string
   342→	if len(sessionsWithContent) > 0 {
   343→		firstSession := sessionsWithContent[0]
   344→		if firstSession.AgentType != "" {
   345→			agentType = firstSession.AgentType
   346→		}
   347→		lastPrompt = s.getLastPrompt(repo, firstSession)
   348→	}
   349→
   350→	// Prepare prompt for display: collapse newlines/whitespace, then truncate (rune-safe)
   351→	displayPrompt := stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(lastPrompt), 80, "...")
   352→
   353→	// Add trailer differently based on commit source
   354→	switch source {
   355→	case "message":
   356→		// Using -m or -F: ask user interactively whether to add trailer
   357→		// (comments won't be stripped by git in this mode)
   358→
   359→		// Build context string for interactive prompt
   360→		var promptContext string
   361→		if displayPrompt != "" {
   362→			promptContext = "You have an active " + string(agentType) + " session.\nLast Prompt: " + displayPrompt
   363→		}
   364→
   365→		if !askConfirmTTY("Link this commit to "+string(agentType)+" session context?", promptContext, true) {
   366→			// User declined - don't add trailer
   367→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: user declined trailer",
   368→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   369→				slog.String("source", source),
   370→			)
   371→			return nil
   372→		}
   373→		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, checkpointID)
   374→	default:
   375→		// Normal editor flow: add trailer with explanatory comment (will be stripped by git)
   376→		message = addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message, checkpointID, string(agentType), displayPrompt)
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer added",
   380→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   381→		slog.String("source", source),
   382→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   383→	)
   384→
   385→	// Write updated message back
   386→	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
   387→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	return nil
   391→}
   392→
   393→// handleAmendCommitMsg handles the prepare-commit-msg hook for amend operations
   394→// (source="commit"). It preserves existing trailers or restores from LastCheckpointID.
   395→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) handleAmendCommitMsg(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string) error {
   396→	// Read current commit message
   397→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   398→	if err != nil {
   399→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   400→	}
   401→
   402→	message := string(content)
   403→
   404→	// If message already has a trailer, keep it unchanged
   405→	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
   406→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: amend preserves existing trailer",
   407→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   408→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
   409→		)
   410→		return nil
   411→	}
   412→
   413→	// No trailer in message — check if any session has LastCheckpointID to restore
   414→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   415→	if err != nil {
   416→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   417→	}
   418→
   419→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   420→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   421→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No sessions - nothing to restore
   422→	}
   423→
   424→	// For amend, HEAD^ is the commit being amended, and HEAD is where we are now.
   425→	// We need to match sessions whose BaseCommit equals HEAD (the commit being amended
   426→	// was created from this base). This prevents stale sessions from injecting
   427→	// unrelated checkpoint IDs.
   428→	repo, repoErr := OpenRepository()
   429→	if repoErr != nil {
   430→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   431→	}
   432→	head, headErr := repo.Head()
   433→	if headErr != nil {
   434→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   435→	}
   436→	currentHead := head.Hash().String()
   437→
   438→	// Find first matching session with LastCheckpointID to restore.
   439→	// LastCheckpointID is set after condensation completes.
   440→	for _, state := range sessions {
   441→		if state.BaseCommit != currentHead {
   442→			continue
   443→		}
   444→		if state.LastCheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   445→			continue
   446→		}
   447→		cpID := state.LastCheckpointID
   448→		source := "LastCheckpointID"
   449→
   450→		// Restore the trailer
   451→		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, cpID)
   452→		if writeErr := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); writeErr != nil {
   453→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   454→		}
   455→
   456→		logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: restored trailer on amend",
   457→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   458→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   459→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   460→			slog.String("source", source),
   461→		)
   462→		return nil
   463→	}
   464→
   465→	// No checkpoint ID found - leave message unchanged
   466→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: amend with no checkpoint to restore",
   467→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   468→	)
   469→	return nil
   470→}
   471→
   472→// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.
   473→// Uses the session state machine to determine what action to take per session:
   474→//   - ACTIVE → condense immediately (each commit gets its own checkpoint)
   475→//   - IDLE → condense immediately
   476→//   - ENDED → condense if files touched, discard if empty
   477→//
   478→// After condensation for ACTIVE sessions, remaining uncommitted files are
   479→// carried forward to a new shadow branch so the next commit gets its own checkpoint.
   480→//
   481→// Shadow branches are only deleted when ALL sessions sharing the branch are non-active
   482→// and were condensed during this PostCommit.
   483→
   484→// postCommitActionHandler implements session.ActionHandler for PostCommit.
   485→// Each session in the loop gets its own handler with per-session context.
   486→// Handler methods use the *State parameter from ApplyTransition (same pointer
   487→// as the state being transitioned) rather than capturing state separately.
   488→type postCommitActionHandler struct {
   489→	s                      *ManualCommitStrategy
   490→	logCtx                 context.Context
   491→	repo                   *git.Repository
   492→	checkpointID           id.CheckpointID
   493→	head                   *plumbing.Reference
   494→	commit                 *object.Commit
   495→	newHead                string
   496→	shadowBranchName       string
   497→	shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{}
   498→	committedFileSet       map[string]struct{}
   499→	hasNew                 bool
   500→	filesTouchedBefore     []string
   501→
   502→	// Output: set by handler methods, read by caller after TransitionAndLog.
   503→	condensed bool
   504→}
   505→
   506→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondense(state *session.State) error {
   507→	shouldCondense := h.shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(state.Phase.IsActive())
   508→
   509→	logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: HandleCondense decision",
   510→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   511→		slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   512→		slog.Bool("has_new", h.hasNew),
   513→		slog.Bool("should_condense", shouldCondense),
   514→		slog.String("shadow_branch", h.shadowBranchName),
   515→	)
   516→
   517→	if shouldCondense {
   518→		h.condensed = h.s.condenseAndUpdateState(h.logCtx, h.repo, h.checkpointID, state, h.head, h.shadowBranchName, h.shadowBranchesToDelete, h.committedFileSet)
   519→	} else {
   520→		h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)
   521→	}
   522→	return nil
   523→}
   524→
   525→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched(state *session.State) error {
   526→	shouldCondense := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(state.Phase.IsActive())
   527→
   528→	logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched decision",
   529→		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   530→		slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   531→		slog.Bool("has_new", h.hasNew),
   532→		slog.Int("files_touched", len(state.FilesTouched)),
   533→		slog.Bool("should_condense", shouldCondense),
   534→		slog.String("shadow_branch", h.shadowBranchName),
   535→	)
   536→
   537→	if shouldCondense {
   538→		h.condensed = h.s.condenseAndUpdateState(h.logCtx, h.repo, h.checkpointID, state, h.head, h.shadowBranchName, h.shadowBranchesToDelete, h.committedFileSet)
   539→	} else {
   540→		h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)
   541→	}
   542→	return nil
   543→}
   544→
   545→// shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck returns true if the session should be condensed
   546→// into this commit. Requires both that hasNew is true AND that the session's files
   547→// overlap with the committed files with matching content.
   548→//
   549→// This prevents stale sessions (ACTIVE sessions where the agent was killed, or
   550→// ENDED/IDLE sessions with carry-forward files) from being condensed into every
   551→// unrelated commit.
   552→//
   553→// filesTouchedBefore is populated from:
   554→//   - state.FilesTouched for IDLE/ENDED sessions (set via SaveStep/SaveTaskStep -> mergeFilesTouched)
   555→//   - transcript extraction for ACTIVE sessions when FilesTouched is empty
   556→//
   557→// When filesTouchedBefore is empty:
   558→//   - For ACTIVE sessions: fail-open (trust hasNew) because the agent may be
   559→//     mid-turn before any files are saved to state.
   560→//   - For IDLE/ENDED sessions: return false because there are no files to
   561→//     overlap with the commit.
   562→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck(isActive bool) bool {
   563→	if !h.hasNew {
   564→		return false
   565→	}
   566→	if len(h.filesTouchedBefore) == 0 {
   567→		return isActive // ACTIVE: fail-open; IDLE/ENDED: no files = no overlap
   568→	}
   569→	// Only check files that were actually changed in this commit.
   570→	// Without this, files that exist in the tree but weren't changed
   571→	// would pass the "modified file" check in filesOverlapWithContent
   572→	// (because the file exists in the parent tree), causing stale
   573→	// sessions to be incorrectly condensed.
   574→	var committedTouchedFiles []string
   575→	for _, f := range h.filesTouchedBefore {
   576→		if _, ok := h.committedFileSet[f]; ok {
   577→			committedTouchedFiles = append(committedTouchedFiles, f)
   578→		}
   579→	}
   580→	if len(committedTouchedFiles) == 0 {
   581→		return false
   582→	}
   583→	return filesOverlapWithContent(h.repo, h.shadowBranchName, h.commit, committedTouchedFiles)
   584→}
   585→
   586→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleDiscardIfNoFiles(state *session.State) error {
   587→	if len(state.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   588→		logging.Debug(h.logCtx, "post-commit: skipping empty ended session (no files to condense)",
   589→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   590→		)
   591→	}
   592→	h.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)
   593→	return nil
   594→}
   595→
   596→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleWarnStaleSession(_ *session.State) error {
   597→	// Not produced by EventGitCommit; no-op for exhaustiveness.
   598→	return nil
   599→}
   600→
   601→// During rebase/cherry-pick/revert operations, phase transitions are skipped entirely.
   602→//
   603→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
   604→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
   605→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   606→
   607→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   608→	if err != nil {
   609→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   610→	}
   611→
   612→	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
   613→	head, err := repo.Head()
   614→	if err != nil {
   615→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   616→	}
   617→
   618→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   619→	if err != nil {
   620→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   621→	}
   622→
   623→	// Check if commit has checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   624→	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
   625→	if !found {
   626→		// No trailer — user removed it or it was never added (mid-turn commit).
   627→		// Still update BaseCommit for active sessions so future commits can match.
   628→		s.postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly(logCtx, head)
   629→		return nil
   630→	}
   631→
   632→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   633→	if err != nil {
   634→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   635→	}
   636→
   637→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   638→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   639→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   640→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: no active sessions despite trailer",
   641→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   642→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   643→		)
   644→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   645→	}
   646→
   647→	// Build transition context
   648→	isRebase := isGitSequenceOperation()
   649→	transitionCtx := session.TransitionContext{
   650→		IsRebaseInProgress: isRebase,
   651→	}
   652→
   653→	if isRebase {
   654→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: rebase/sequence in progress, skipping phase transitions",
   655→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   656→		)
   657→	}
   658→
   659→	// Track shadow branch names and whether they can be deleted
   660→	shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})
   661→	// Track active sessions that were NOT condensed — their shadow branches must be preserved
   662→	uncondensedActiveOnBranch := make(map[string]bool)
   663→
   664→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   665→	committedFileSet := filesChangedInCommit(commit)
   666→
   667→	for _, state := range sessions {
   668→		shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   669→
   670→		// Check for new content (needed for TransitionContext and condensation).
   671→		// Fail-open: if content check errors, assume new content exists so we
   672→		// don't silently skip data that should have been condensed.
   673→		//
   674→		// For ACTIVE sessions: the commit has a checkpoint trailer (verified above),
   675→		// meaning PrepareCommitMsg already determined this commit is session-related.
   676→		// The trailer is only added when either:
   677→		//   - No TTY (agent/subagent committing) — added unconditionally
   678→		//   - TTY (human committing) — added after content detection confirmed agent work
   679→		// In both cases, PrepareCommitMsg already validated this commit. We trust
   680→		// that decision here. Transcript-based re-validation is unreliable because
   681→		// subagent transcripts may not be available yet (subagent still running).
   682→		var hasNew bool
   683→		if state.Phase.IsActive() {
   684→			hasNew = true
   685→		} else {
   686→			var contentErr error
   687→			hasNew, contentErr = s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   688→			if contentErr != nil {
   689→				hasNew = true
   690→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: error checking session content, assuming new content",
   691→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   692→					slog.String("error", contentErr.Error()),
   693→				)
   694→			}
   695→		}
   696→		transitionCtx.HasFilesTouched = len(state.FilesTouched) > 0
   697→
   698→		// Save FilesTouched BEFORE TransitionAndLog — the handler's condensation
   699→		// clears it, but we need the original list for carry-forward computation.
   700→		// For mid-session commits (ACTIVE, no shadow branch), state.FilesTouched may be empty
   701→		// because no SaveStep/Stop has been called yet. Extract files from transcript.
   702→		filesTouchedBefore := make([]string, len(state.FilesTouched))
   703→		copy(filesTouchedBefore, state.FilesTouched)
   704→		if len(filesTouchedBefore) == 0 && state.Phase.IsActive() && state.TranscriptPath != "" {
   705→			filesTouchedBefore = s.extractFilesFromLiveTranscript(state)
   706→		}
   707→
   708→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward prep",
   709→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   710→			slog.Bool("is_active", state.Phase.IsActive()),
   711→			slog.String("transcript_path", state.TranscriptPath),
   712→			slog.Int("files_touched_before", len(filesTouchedBefore)),
   713→			slog.Any("files", filesTouchedBefore),
   714→		)
   715→
   716→		// Run the state machine transition with handler for strategy-specific actions.
   717→		handler := &postCommitActionHandler{
   718→			s:                      s,
   719→			logCtx:                 logCtx,
   720→			repo:                   repo,
   721→			checkpointID:           checkpointID,
   722→			head:                   head,
   723→			commit:                 commit,
   724→			newHead:                newHead,
   725→			shadowBranchName:       shadowBranchName,
   726→			shadowBranchesToDelete: shadowBranchesToDelete,
   727→			committedFileSet:       committedFileSet,
   728→			hasNew:                 hasNew,
   729→			filesTouchedBefore:     filesTouchedBefore,
   730→		}
   731→
   732→		if err := TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventGitCommit, transitionCtx, handler); err != nil {
   733→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: post-commit action handler error: %v\n", err)
   734→		}
   735→
   736→		// Record checkpoint ID for ACTIVE sessions so HandleTurnEnd can finalize
   737→		// with full transcript. IDLE/ENDED sessions already have complete transcripts.
   738→		// NOTE: This check runs AFTER TransitionAndLog updated the phase. It relies on
   739→		// ACTIVE + GitCommit → ACTIVE (phase stays ACTIVE). If that state machine
   740→		// transition ever changed, this guard would silently stop recording IDs.
   741→		if handler.condensed && state.Phase.IsActive() {
   742→			state.TurnCheckpointIDs = append(state.TurnCheckpointIDs, checkpointID.String())
   743→		}
   744→
   745→		// Carry forward remaining uncommitted files so the next commit gets its
   746→		// own checkpoint ID. This applies to ALL phases — if a user splits their
   747→		// commit across two `git commit` invocations, each gets a 1:1 checkpoint.
   748→		// Uses content-aware comparison: if user did `git add -p` and committed
   749→		// partial changes, the file still has remaining agent changes to carry forward.
   750→		if handler.condensed {
   751→			remainingFiles := filesWithRemainingAgentChanges(repo, shadowBranchName, commit, filesTouchedBefore, committedFileSet)
   752→			state.FilesTouched = remainingFiles
   753→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: carry-forward decision (content-aware)",
   754→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   755→				slog.Int("files_touched_before", len(filesTouchedBefore)),
   756→				slog.Int("committed_files", len(committedFileSet)),
   757→				slog.Int("remaining_files", len(remainingFiles)),
   758→				slog.Any("remaining", remainingFiles),
   759→				slog.Any("committed_files", committedFileSet),
   760→			)
   761→			if len(remainingFiles) > 0 {
   762→				s.carryForwardToNewShadowBranch(logCtx, repo, state, remainingFiles)
   763→			}
   764→		}
   765→
   766→		// Save the updated state
   767→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   768→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   769→		}
   770→
   771→		// Only preserve shadow branch for active sessions that were NOT condensed.
   772→		// Condensed sessions already have their data on entire/checkpoints/v1.
   773→		if state.Phase.IsActive() && !handler.condensed {
   774→			uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
   775→		}
   776→	}
   777→
   778→	// Clean up shadow branches — only delete when ALL sessions on the branch are non-active
   779→	// or were condensed during this PostCommit.
   780→	for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
   781→		if uncondensedActiveOnBranch[shadowBranchName] {
   782→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: preserving shadow branch (active session exists)",
   783→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   784→			)
   785→			continue
   786→		}
   787→		if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   788→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\n", shadowBranchName, err)
   789→		} else {
   790→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", shadowBranchName)
   791→			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted",
   792→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   793→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   794→			)
   795→		}
   796→	}
   797→
   798→	return nil
   799→}
   800→
   801→// condenseAndUpdateState runs condensation for a session and updates state afterward.
   802→// Returns true if condensation succeeded.
   803→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
   804→	logCtx context.Context,
   805→	repo *git.Repository,
   806→	checkpointID id.CheckpointID,
   807→	state *SessionState,
   808→	head *plumbing.Reference,
   809→	shadowBranchName string,
   810→	shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{},
   811→	committedFiles map[string]struct{},
   812→) bool {
   813→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state, committedFiles)
   814→	if err != nil {
   815→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: condensation failed for session %s: %v\n",
   816→			state.SessionID, err)
   817→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: condensation failed",
   818→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   819→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   820→		)
   821→		return false
   822→	}
   823→
   824→	// Link checkpoint to trail (best-effort)
   825→	appendCheckpointToTrail(repo, result.CheckpointID, head.Hash(), result.Prompts)
   826→
   827→	// Track this shadow branch for cleanup
   828→	shadowBranchesToDelete[shadowBranchName] = struct{}{}
   829→
   830→	// Update session state for the new base commit
   831→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   832→	state.BaseCommit = newHead
   833→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = newHead
   834→	state.StepCount = 0
   835→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   836→
   837→	// Clear attribution tracking — condensation already used these values
   838→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   839→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   840→	state.FilesTouched = nil
   841→
   842→	// Save checkpoint ID so subsequent commits can reuse it (e.g., amend restores trailer)
   843→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   844→
   845→	shortID := state.SessionID
   846→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   847→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   848→	}
   849→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Condensed session %s: %s (%d checkpoints)\n",
   850→		shortID, result.CheckpointID, result.CheckpointsCount)
   851→
   852→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session condensed",
   853→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   854→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   855→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   856→		slog.Int("transcript_lines", result.TotalTranscriptLines),
   857→	)
   858→
   859→	return true
   860→}
   861→
   862→// updateBaseCommitIfChanged updates BaseCommit to newHead if it changed.
   863→// Only updates ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions should NOT have their
   864→// BaseCommit updated, as this would cause them to be incorrectly associated
   865→// with a new shadow branch and potentially condensed on future commits.
   866→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx context.Context, state *SessionState, newHead string) {
   867→	// Only update ACTIVE sessions. IDLE/ENDED sessions are kept around for
   868→	// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
   869→	if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   870→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updateBaseCommitIfChanged skipped non-active session",
   871→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   872→			slog.String("phase", string(state.Phase)),
   873→		)
   874→		return
   875→	}
   876→	if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   877→		state.BaseCommit = newHead
   878→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updated BaseCommit",
   879→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   880→			slog.String("new_head", truncateHash(newHead)),
   881→		)
   882→	}
   883→}
   884→
   885→// postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly updates BaseCommit for all sessions on the current
   886→// worktree when a commit has no Entire-Checkpoint trailer. This prevents BaseCommit
   887→// from going stale, which would cause future PrepareCommitMsg calls to skip the
   888→// session (BaseCommit != currentHeadHash filter).
   889→//
   890→// Unlike the full PostCommit flow, this does NOT fire EventGitCommit or trigger
   891→// condensation — it only keeps BaseCommit in sync with HEAD.
   892→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) postCommitUpdateBaseCommitOnly(logCtx context.Context, head *plumbing.Reference) {
   893→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   894→	if err != nil {
   895→		return // Silent failure — hooks must be resilient
   896→	}
   897→
   898→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   899→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   900→		return
   901→	}
   902→
   903→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   904→	for _, state := range sessions {
   905→		// Only update active sessions. Idle/ended sessions are kept around for
   906→		// LastCheckpointID reuse and should not be advanced to HEAD.
   907→		if !state.Phase.IsActive() {
   908→			continue
   909→		}
   910→		if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   911→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit (no trailer): updating BaseCommit",
   912→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   913→				slog.String("old_base", truncateHash(state.BaseCommit)),
   914→				slog.String("new_head", truncateHash(newHead)),
   915→			)
   916→			state.BaseCommit = newHead
   917→			if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   918→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   919→			}
   920→		}
   921→	}
   922→}
   923→
   924→// truncateHash safely truncates a git hash to 7 chars for logging.
   925→func truncateHash(h string) string {
   926→	if len(h) > 7 {
   927→		return h[:7]
   928→	}
   929→	return h
   930→}
   931→
   932→// filterSessionsWithNewContent returns sessions that have new transcript content
   933→// beyond what was already condensed.
   934→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo *git.Repository, sessions []*SessionState) []*SessionState {
   935→	var result []*SessionState
   936→
   937→	for _, state := range sessions {
   938→		hasNew, err := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   939→		if err != nil {
   940→			// On error, include the session (fail open for hooks)
   941→			result = append(result, state)
   942→			continue
   943→		}
   944→		if hasNew {
   945→			result = append(result, state)
   946→		}
   947→	}
   948→
   949→	return result
   950→}
   951→
   952→// sessionHasNewContent checks if a session has new transcript content
   953→// beyond what was already condensed.
   954→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContent(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   955→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "manual-commit")
   956→
   957→	// Get shadow branch
   958→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   959→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   960→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   961→	if err != nil {
   962→		// No shadow branch means no Stop has happened since the last condensation.
   963→		// However, the agent may have done work (including commits) without a Stop.
   964→		// Check the live transcript to detect this scenario.
   965→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no shadow branch, checking live transcript",
   966→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   967→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   968→		)
   969→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
   970→	}
   971→
   972→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   973→	if err != nil {
   974→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   975→	}
   976→
   977→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   978→	if err != nil {
   979→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   980→	}
   981→
   982→	// Look for transcript file
   983→	metadataDir := paths.EntireMetadataDir + "/" + state.SessionID
   984→	var transcriptLines int
   985→	var hasTranscriptFile bool
   986→
   987→	if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   988→		hasTranscriptFile = true
   989→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   990→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, content)
   991→		}
   992→	} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   993→		hasTranscriptFile = true
   994→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   995→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, content)
   996→		}
   997→	}
   998→
   999→	// If shadow branch exists but has no transcript (e.g., carry-forward from mid-session commit),
  1000→	// check if the session has FilesTouched. Carry-forward sets FilesTouched with remaining files.
  1001→	if !hasTranscriptFile {
  1002→		if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 {
  1003→			// Shadow branch has files from carry-forward - check if staged files overlap
  1004→			// AND have matching content (content-aware check).
  1005→			stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
  1006→			if len(stagedFiles) > 0 {
  1007→				// PrepareCommitMsg context: check staged files overlap with content
  1008→				result := stagedFilesOverlapWithContent(repo, tree, stagedFiles, state.FilesTouched)
  1009→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no transcript, carry-forward with staged files",
  1010→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1011→					slog.Int("files_touched", len(state.FilesTouched)),
  1012→					slog.Int("staged_files", len(stagedFiles)),
  1013→					slog.Bool("result", result),
  1014→				)
  1015→				return result, nil
  1016→			}
  1017→			// PostCommit context: no staged files, but we have carry-forward files.
  1018→			// Return true and let the caller do the overlap check with committed files.
  1019→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no transcript, carry-forward without staged files (post-commit context)",
  1020→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1021→				slog.Int("files_touched", len(state.FilesTouched)),
  1022→			)
  1023→			return true, nil
  1024→		}
  1025→		// No transcript and no FilesTouched - fall back to live transcript check
  1026→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "sessionHasNewContent: no transcript and no files touched, checking live transcript",
  1027→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
  1028→		)
  1029→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
  1030→	}
  1031→
  1032→	// Check if there's new content to condense. Two cases:
