    27→
    28→// isNotFoundError checks if an error represents a "not found" condition in go-git.
    29→// This includes entry not found, file not found, directory not found, and object not found.
    30→func isNotFoundError(err error) bool {
    31→	return errors.Is(err, object.ErrEntryNotFound) ||
    32→		errors.Is(err, object.ErrFileNotFound) ||
    33→		errors.Is(err, object.ErrDirectoryNotFound) ||
    34→		errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrObjectNotFound) ||
    35→		errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound)
    36→}
    37→
    38→// commitOrHead attempts to create a commit. If the commit would be empty (files already
    39→// committed), it returns HEAD hash instead. This handles the case where files were
    40→// modified during a session but already committed by the user before the hook runs.
    41→func commitOrHead(repo *git.Repository, worktree *git.Worktree, msg string, author *object.Signature) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
    42→	commitHash, err := worktree.Commit(msg, &git.CommitOptions{Author: author})
    43→	if errors.Is(err, git.ErrEmptyCommit) {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No changes to commit (files already committed)\n")
    45→		head, err := repo.Head()
    46→		if err != nil {
    47→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    48→		}
    49→		return head.Hash(), nil
    50→	}
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to commit: %w", err)
    53→	}
    54→	return commitHash, nil
    55→}
    56→
    57→// AutoCommitStrategy implements the auto-commit strategy:
    58→// - Code changes are committed to the active branch (like commit strategy)
    59→// - Session logs are committed to a shadow branch (like manual-commit strategy)
    60→// - Code commits can reference the shadow branch via trailers
    61→type AutoCommitStrategy struct {
    62→	// checkpointStore manages checkpoint data on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
    63→	checkpointStore *checkpoint.GitStore
    64→	// checkpointStoreOnce ensures thread-safe lazy initialization
    65→	checkpointStoreOnce sync.Once
    66→	// checkpointStoreErr captures any error during initialization
    67→	checkpointStoreErr error
    68→}
    69→
    70→// getCheckpointStore returns the checkpoint store, initializing it lazily if needed.
    71→// Thread-safe via sync.Once.
    72→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) getCheckpointStore() (*checkpoint.GitStore, error) {
    73→	s.checkpointStoreOnce.Do(func() {
    74→		repo, err := OpenRepository()
    75→		if err != nil {
    76→			s.checkpointStoreErr = fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
    77→			return
    78→		}
    79→		s.checkpointStore = checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
    80→	})
    81→	return s.checkpointStore, s.checkpointStoreErr
    82→}
    83→
    84→// NewAutoCommitStrategy creates a new AutoCommitStrategy instance.
    85→//
    86→
    87→func NewAutoCommitStrategy() Strategy {
    88→	return &AutoCommitStrategy{}
    89→}
    90→
    91→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Name() string {
    92→	return StrategyNameAutoCommit
    93→}
    94→
    95→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Description() string {
    96→	return "Auto-commits code to active branch with metadata on entire/checkpoints/v1"
    97→}
    98→
    99→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) ValidateRepository() error {
   100→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		return fmt.Errorf("not a git repository: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	_, err = repo.Worktree()
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to access worktree: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	return nil
   111→}
   112→
   113→// PrePush is called by the git pre-push hook before pushing to a remote.
   114→// It pushes the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch alongside the user's push.
   115→// Configuration options (stored in .entire/settings.json under strategy_options.push_sessions):
   116→//   - "auto": always push automatically
   117→//   - "prompt" (default): ask user with option to enable auto
   118→//   - "false"/"off"/"no": never push
   119→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) PrePush(remote string) error {
   120→	if err := pushSessionsBranchCommon(remote, paths.MetadataBranchName); err != nil {
   121→		return err
   122→	}
   123→	return PushTrailsBranch(remote)
   124→}
   125→
   126→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) SaveStep(ctx StepContext) error {
   127→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   128→	if err != nil {
   129→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	// Generate checkpoint ID for this commit
   133→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
   134→	if err != nil {
   135→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   136→	}
   137→	// Step 1: Commit code changes to active branch with checkpoint ID trailer
   138→	// We do code first to avoid orphaned metadata if this step fails.
   139→	// If metadata commit fails after this, the code commit exists but GetRewindPoints
   140→	// already handles missing metadata gracefully (skips commits without metadata).
   141→	codeResult, err := s.commitCodeToActive(repo, ctx, cpID)
   142→	if err != nil {
   143→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit code to active branch: %w", err)
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	// If no code commit was created (no changes), skip metadata creation
   147→	// This prevents orphaned metadata commits that don't correspond to any code commit
   148→	if !codeResult.Created {
   149→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   150→		logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint skipped (no changes)",
   151→			slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   152→			slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   153→		)
   154→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipped checkpoint (no changes since last commit)\n")
   155→		return nil
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Step 2: Commit metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch using sharded path
   159→	// Path is <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/ for direct lookup
   160→	_, err = s.commitMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo, ctx, cpID)
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch: %w", err)
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// Link checkpoint to trail (best-effort)
   166→	appendCheckpointToTrail(repo, cpID, codeResult.CommitHash, nil)
   167→
   168→	// Log checkpoint creation
   169→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   170→	logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint saved",
   171→		slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   172→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   173→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   174→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   175→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   176→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   177→	)
   178→
   179→	return nil
   180→}
   181→
   182→// commitCodeResult contains the result of committing code to the active branch.
   183→type commitCodeResult struct {
   184→	CommitHash plumbing.Hash
   185→	Created    bool // True if a new commit was created, false if skipped (no changes)
   186→}
   187→
   188→// commitCodeToActive commits code changes to the active branch.
   189→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer for metadata lookup that survives amend/rebase.
   190→// Returns the result containing commit hash and whether a commit was created.
   191→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitCodeToActive(repo *git.Repository, ctx StepContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (commitCodeResult, error) {
   192→	// Check if there are any code changes to commit
   193→	if len(ctx.ModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(ctx.NewFiles) == 0 && len(ctx.DeletedFiles) == 0 {
   194→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No code changes to commit to active branch\n")
   195→		// Return current HEAD hash but mark as not created
   196→		head, err := repo.Head()
   197→		if err != nil {
   198→			return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   199→		}
   200→		return commitCodeResult{CommitHash: head.Hash(), Created: false}, nil
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   204→	if err != nil {
   205→		return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	// Get HEAD hash before commit to detect if commitOrHead actually creates a new commit
   209→	// (commitOrHead returns HEAD hash without error when git.ErrEmptyCommit occurs)
   210→	headBefore, err := repo.Head()
   211→	if err != nil {
   212→		return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Stage code changes
   216→	StageFiles(worktree, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles, StageForSession)
   217→
   218→	// Add checkpoint ID trailer to commit message
   219→	commitMsg := ctx.CommitMessage + "\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   220→
   221→	author := &object.Signature{
   222→		Name:  ctx.AuthorName,
   223→		Email: ctx.AuthorEmail,
   224→		When:  time.Now(),
   225→	}
   226→	commitHash, err := commitOrHead(repo, worktree, commitMsg, author)
   227→	if err != nil {
   228→		return commitCodeResult{}, err
   229→	}
   230→
   231→	// Check if a new commit was actually created by comparing with HEAD before
   232→	created := commitHash != headBefore.Hash()
   233→	if created {
   234→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed code changes to active branch (%s)\n", commitHash.String()[:7])
   235→	}
   236→	return commitCodeResult{CommitHash: commitHash, Created: created}, nil
   237→}
   238→
   239→// commitMetadataToMetadataBranch commits session metadata to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   240→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/
   241→// This allows direct lookup from the checkpoint ID trailer on the code commit.
   242→// Uses checkpoint.WriteCommitted for git operations.
   243→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo *git.Repository, ctx StepContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   244→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   245→	if err != nil {
   246→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	// Extract session ID from metadata dir
   250→	sessionID := filepath.Base(ctx.MetadataDir)
   251→
   252→	// Get current branch name
   253→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   254→
   255→	// Combine all file changes into FilesTouched (same as manual-commit)
   256→	filesTouched := mergeFilesTouched(nil, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)
   257→
   258→	// Load TurnID from session state (correlates checkpoints from the same turn)
   259→	var turnID string
   260→	if state, loadErr := LoadSessionState(sessionID); loadErr == nil && state != nil {
   261→		turnID = state.TurnID
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	// Write committed checkpoint using the checkpoint store
   265→	// Pass TranscriptPath so writeTranscript generates content_hash.txt
   266→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(ctx.MetadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   267→	err = store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteCommittedOptions{
   268→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   269→		SessionID:                   sessionID,
   270→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameAutoCommit, // Use new strategy name
   271→		Branch:                      branchName,
   272→		MetadataDir:                 ctx.MetadataDirAbs, // Copy all files from metadata dir
   273→		TranscriptPath:              transcriptPath,     // For content hash generation
   274→		AuthorName:                  ctx.AuthorName,
   275→		AuthorEmail:                 ctx.AuthorEmail,
   276→		Agent:                       ctx.AgentType,
   277→		TurnID:                      turnID,
   278→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: ctx.StepTranscriptIdentifier,
   279→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   ctx.StepTranscriptStart,
   280→		TokenUsage:                  ctx.TokenUsage,
   281→		CheckpointsCount:            1,            // Each auto-commit checkpoint = 1
   282→		FilesTouched:                filesTouched, // Track modified files (same as manual-commit)
   283→	})
   284→	if err != nil {
   285→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to write committed checkpoint: %w", err)
   286→	}
   287→
   288→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed session metadata to %s (%s)\n", paths.MetadataBranchName, checkpointID)
   289→	return plumbing.ZeroHash, nil // Commit hash not needed by callers
   290→}
   291→
   292→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error) {
   293→	// For auto-commit strategy, rewind points are found by looking for Entire-Checkpoint trailers
   294→	// in the current branch's commit history. The checkpoint ID provides direct lookup
   295→	// to metadata on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   296→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   297→	if err != nil {
   298→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	head, err := repo.Head()
   302→	if err != nil {
   303→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// Get metadata branch tree for lookups
   307→	metadataTree, err := GetMetadataBranchTree(repo)
   308→	if err != nil {
   309→		// No metadata branch yet is fine
   310→		return []RewindPoint{}, nil //nolint:nilerr // Expected when no metadata exists
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	// Get the main branch commit hash to determine branch-only commits
   314→	mainBranchHash := GetMainBranchHash(repo)
   315→
   316→	// Walk current branch history looking for commits with checkpoint trailers
   317→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{
   318→		From:  head.Hash(),
   319→		Order: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,
   320→	})
   321→	if err != nil {
   322→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit log: %w", err)
   323→	}
   324→
   325→	var points []RewindPoint
   326→	count := 0
   327→
   328→	err = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   329→		if count >= logsOnlyScanLimit || len(points) >= limit {
   330→			return errStop
   331→		}
   332→		count++
   333→
   334→		// Check for Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   335→		cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message)
   336→		if !found {
   337→			return nil
   338→		}
   339→
   340→		// Look up metadata from sharded path
   341→		checkpointPath := cpID.Path()
   342→		metadata, err := ReadCheckpointMetadata(metadataTree, checkpointPath)
   343→		if err != nil {
   344→			// Checkpoint exists in commit but no metadata found - skip this commit
   345→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: skip commits without metadata
   346→		}
   347→
   348→		message := strings.Split(c.Message, "\n")[0]
   349→
   350→		// Determine if this is a full rewind or logs-only
   351→		// Full rewind is allowed if commit is only on this branch (not reachable from main)
   352→		isLogsOnly := false
   353→		if mainBranchHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {
   354→			if IsAncestorOf(repo, c.Hash, mainBranchHash) {
   355→				isLogsOnly = true
   356→			}
   357→		}
   358→
   359→		// Build metadata path - for task checkpoints, include the task path
   360→		metadataDir := checkpointPath
   361→		if metadata.IsTask && metadata.ToolUseID != "" {
   362→			metadataDir = checkpointPath + "/tasks/" + metadata.ToolUseID
   363→		}
   364→
   365→		// Read session prompt from metadata tree
   366→		sessionPrompt := ReadSessionPromptFromTree(metadataTree, checkpointPath)
   367→
   368→		points = append(points, RewindPoint{
   369→			ID:               c.Hash.String(),
   370→			Message:          message,
   371→			MetadataDir:      metadataDir,
   372→			Date:             c.Author.When,
   373→			IsLogsOnly:       isLogsOnly,
   374→			CheckpointID:     cpID,
   375→			IsTaskCheckpoint: metadata.IsTask,
   376→			ToolUseID:        metadata.ToolUseID,
   377→			Agent:            metadata.Agent,
   378→			SessionID:        metadata.SessionID,
   379→			SessionPrompt:    sessionPrompt,
   380→		})
   381→
   382→		return nil
   383→	})
   384→
   385→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStop) {
   386→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to iterate commits: %w", err)
   387→	}
   388→
   389→	return points, nil
   390→}
   391→
   392→// findTaskMetadataPathForCommit looks up the task metadata path for a task checkpoint commit
   393→// by searching the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch commit history for the checkpoint directory.
   394→// Returns ("", nil) if metadata is not found - this is expected for commits without metadata.
   395→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) findTaskMetadataPathForCommit(repo *git.Repository, commitSHA, toolUseID string) (string, error) {
   396→	// Get the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   397→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   398→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   399→	if err != nil {
   400→		if isNotFoundError(err) {
   401→			return "", nil // No metadata branch yet
   402→		}
   403→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata branch: %w", err)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	// Search commit history for a commit referencing this code commit SHA and tool use ID
   407→	shortSHA := commitSHA
   408→	if len(shortSHA) > 7 {
   409→		shortSHA = shortSHA[:7]
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: ref.Hash()})
   413→	if err != nil {
   414→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit log: %w", err)
   415→	}
   416→
   417→	var foundTaskPath string
   418→	err = iter.ForEach(func(commit *object.Commit) error {
   419→		// Check if commit message contains "Commit: <sha>" and the tool use ID
   420→		if strings.Contains(commit.Message, "Commit: "+shortSHA) &&
   421→			strings.Contains(commit.Message, toolUseID) {
   422→			// Parse task metadata trailer
   423→			if taskPath, found := trailers.ParseTaskMetadata(commit.Message); found {
   424→				foundTaskPath = taskPath
   425→				return errStop // Found it
   426→			}
   427→		}
   428→		return nil
   429→	})
   430→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStop) {
   431→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to iterate commits: %w", err)
   432→	}
   433→
   434→	return foundTaskPath, nil
   435→}
   436→
   437→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Rewind(point RewindPoint) error {
   438→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
   439→	shortID, err := HardResetWithProtection(commitHash)
   440→	if err != nil {
   441→		return err
   442→	}
   443→
   444→	fmt.Println()
   445→	fmt.Printf("Reset to commit %s\n", shortID)
   446→	fmt.Println()
   447→
   448→	return nil
   449→}
   450→
   451→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) CanRewind() (bool, string, error) {
   452→	return checkCanRewind()
   453→}
   454→
   455→// PreviewRewind returns what will happen if rewinding to the given point.
   456→// For auto-commit strategy, this returns nil since git reset doesn't delete untracked files.
   457→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) PreviewRewind(_ RewindPoint) (*RewindPreview, error) {
   458→	// Auto-commit uses git reset --hard which doesn't affect untracked files
   459→	// Return empty preview to indicate no untracked files will be deleted
   460→	return &RewindPreview{}, nil
   461→}
   462→
   463→// EnsureSetup ensures the strategy's required setup is in place.
   464→// For auto-commit strategy:
   465→// - Ensure .entire/.gitignore has all required entries
   466→// - Create orphan entire/checkpoints/v1 branch if it doesn't exist
   467→// - Install git hooks if missing (self-healing for third-party overwrites)
   468→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) EnsureSetup() error {
   469→	if err := EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   470→		return err
   471→	}
   472→
   473→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   474→	if err != nil {
   475→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   476→	}
   477→
   478→	// Ensure the entire/checkpoints/v1 orphan branch exists
   479→	if err := EnsureMetadataBranch(repo); err != nil {
   480→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure metadata branch: %w", err)
   481→	}
   482→
   483→	// Install generic hooks if missing (they delegate to strategy at runtime)
   484→	if !IsGitHookInstalled() {
   485→		if _, err := InstallGitHook(true); err != nil {
   486→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to install git hooks: %w", err)
   487→		}
   488→	}
   489→
   490→	return nil
   491→}
   492→
   493→// GetSessionInfo returns session information for linking commits.
   494→// For auto-commit strategy, we don't track active sessions - metadata is stored on
   495→// entire/checkpoints/v1 branch when SaveStep is called. Active branch commits
   496→// are kept clean (no trailers), so this returns ErrNoSession.
   497→// Use ListSessions() or GetSession() to retrieve session info from the metadata branch.
   498→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetSessionInfo() (*SessionInfo, error) {
   499→	// Dual strategy doesn't track active sessions like shadow does.
   500→	// Session metadata is stored on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch and can be
   501→	// retrieved via ListSessions() or GetSession().
   502→	return nil, ErrNoSession
   503→}
   504→
   505→// SaveTaskStep creates a checkpoint commit for a completed task.
   506→// For auto-commit strategy:
   507→// 1. Commit code changes to active branch (no trailers - clean history)
   508→// 2. Commit task metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch with checkpoint format
   509→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) SaveTaskStep(ctx TaskStepContext) error {
   510→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   511→	if err != nil {
   512→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   513→	}
   514→
   515→	// Ensure entire/checkpoints/v1 branch exists
   516→	if err := EnsureMetadataBranch(repo); err != nil {
   517→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure metadata branch: %w", err)
   518→	}
   519→
   520→	// Generate checkpoint ID for this task checkpoint
   521→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
   522→	if err != nil {
   523→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   524→	}
   525→
   526→	// Step 1: Commit code changes to active branch with checkpoint ID trailer
   527→	// We do code first to avoid orphaned metadata if this step fails.
   528→	_, err = s.commitTaskCodeToActive(repo, ctx, cpID)
   529→	if err != nil {
   530→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit task code to active branch: %w", err)
   531→	}
   532→
   533→	// Step 2: Commit task metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch at sharded path
   534→	_, err = s.commitTaskMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo, ctx, cpID)
   535→	if err != nil {
   536→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit task metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch: %w", err)
   537→	}
   538→
   539→	// Log task checkpoint creation
   540→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   541→	attrs := []any{
   542→		slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   543→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "task"),
   544→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   545→		slog.String("checkpoint_uuid", ctx.CheckpointUUID),
   546→		slog.String("tool_use_id", ctx.ToolUseID),
   547→		slog.String("subagent_type", ctx.SubagentType),
   548→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   549→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   550→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   551→	}
   552→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   553→		attrs = append(attrs,
   554→			slog.Bool("is_incremental", true),
   555→			slog.String("incremental_type", ctx.IncrementalType),
   556→			slog.Int("incremental_sequence", ctx.IncrementalSequence),
   557→		)
   558→	}
   559→	logging.Info(logCtx, "task checkpoint saved", attrs...)
   560→
   561→	return nil
   562→}
   563→
   564→// commitTaskCodeToActive commits task code changes to the active branch.
   565→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer for metadata lookup that survives amend/rebase.
   566→// Skips commit creation if there are no file changes.
   567→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitTaskCodeToActive(repo *git.Repository, ctx TaskStepContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   568→	hasFileChanges := len(ctx.ModifiedFiles) > 0 || len(ctx.NewFiles) > 0 || len(ctx.DeletedFiles) > 0
   569→
   570→	// If no file changes, skip code commit
   571→	if !hasFileChanges {
   572→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No code changes to commit for task checkpoint\n")
   573→		// Return current HEAD hash so metadata can still be stored
   574→		head, err := repo.Head()
   575→		if err != nil {
   576→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   577→		}
   578→		return head.Hash(), nil
   579→	}
   580→
   581→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   582→	if err != nil {
   583→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
   584→	}
   585→
   586→	// Stage code changes
   587→	StageFiles(worktree, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles, StageForTask)
   588→
   589→	// Build commit message with checkpoint trailer
   590→	shortToolUseID := ctx.ToolUseID
   591→	if len(shortToolUseID) > id.ShortIDLength {
   592→		shortToolUseID = shortToolUseID[:id.ShortIDLength]
   593→	}
   594→
   595→	var subject string
   596→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   597→		subject = FormatIncrementalSubject(
   598→			ctx.IncrementalType,
   599→			ctx.SubagentType,
   600→			ctx.TaskDescription,
   601→			ctx.TodoContent,
   602→			ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   603→			shortToolUseID,
   604→		)
   605→	} else {
   606→		subject = FormatSubagentEndMessage(ctx.SubagentType, ctx.TaskDescription, shortToolUseID)
   607→	}
   608→
   609→	// Add checkpoint ID trailer to commit message
   610→	commitMsg := subject + "\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   611→
   612→	author := &object.Signature{
   613→		Name:  ctx.AuthorName,
   614→		Email: ctx.AuthorEmail,
   615→		When:  time.Now(),
   616→	}
   617→
   618→	commitHash, err := commitOrHead(repo, worktree, commitMsg, author)
   619→	if err != nil {
   620→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
   621→	}
   622→
   623→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   624→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed incremental checkpoint #%d to active branch (%s)\n", ctx.IncrementalSequence, commitHash.String()[:7])
   625→	} else {
   626→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed task checkpoint to active branch (%s)\n", commitHash.String()[:7])
   627→	}
   628→	return commitHash, nil
   629→}
   630→
   631→// commitTaskMetadataToMetadataBranch commits task metadata to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   632→// Uses sharded path: <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/tasks/<tool-use-id>/
   633→// Returns the metadata commit hash.
   634→// When IsIncremental is true, only writes the incremental checkpoint file, skipping transcripts.
   635→// Uses checkpoint.WriteCommitted for git operations.
   636→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitTaskMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo *git.Repository, ctx TaskStepContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   637→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   638→	if err != nil {
   639→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   640→	}
   641→
   642→	// Format commit subject line for better git log readability
   643→	shortToolUseID := ctx.ToolUseID
   644→	if len(shortToolUseID) > id.ShortIDLength {
   645→		shortToolUseID = shortToolUseID[:id.ShortIDLength]
   646→	}
   647→
   648→	var messageSubject string
   649→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   650→		messageSubject = FormatIncrementalSubject(
   651→			ctx.IncrementalType,
   652→			ctx.SubagentType,
   653→			ctx.TaskDescription,
   654→			ctx.TodoContent,
   655→			ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   656→			shortToolUseID,
   657→		)
   658→	} else {
   659→		messageSubject = FormatSubagentEndMessage(ctx.SubagentType, ctx.TaskDescription, shortToolUseID)
   660→	}
   661→
   662→	// Get current branch name
   663→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   664→
   665→	// Write committed checkpoint using the checkpoint store
   666→	err = store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteCommittedOptions{
   667→		CheckpointID:           checkpointID,
   668→		SessionID:              ctx.SessionID,
   669→		Strategy:               StrategyNameAutoCommit,
   670→		Branch:                 branchName,
   671→		IsTask:                 true,
   672→		ToolUseID:              ctx.ToolUseID,
   673→		AgentID:                ctx.AgentID,
   674→		CheckpointUUID:         ctx.CheckpointUUID,
   675→		TranscriptPath:         ctx.TranscriptPath,
   676→		SubagentTranscriptPath: ctx.SubagentTranscriptPath,
   677→		IsIncremental:          ctx.IsIncremental,
   678→		IncrementalSequence:    ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   679→		IncrementalType:        ctx.IncrementalType,
   680→		IncrementalData:        ctx.IncrementalData,
   681→		CommitSubject:          messageSubject,
   682→		AuthorName:             ctx.AuthorName,
   683→		AuthorEmail:            ctx.AuthorEmail,
   684→		Agent:                  ctx.AgentType,
   685→	})
   686→	if err != nil {
   687→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to write task checkpoint: %w", err)
   688→	}
   689→
   690→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   691→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed incremental checkpoint metadata to %s (%s)\n", paths.MetadataBranchName, checkpointID)
   692→	} else {
   693→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed task metadata to %s (%s)\n", paths.MetadataBranchName, checkpointID)
   694→	}
   695→	return plumbing.ZeroHash, nil // Commit hash not needed by callers
   696→}
   697→
   698→// GetTaskCheckpoint returns the task checkpoint for a given rewind point.
   699→// For auto-commit strategy, checkpoints are stored on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch in checkpoint directories.
   700→// Returns ErrNotTaskCheckpoint if the point is not a task checkpoint.
   701→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetTaskCheckpoint(point RewindPoint) (*TaskCheckpoint, error) {
   702→	if !point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
   703→		return nil, ErrNotTaskCheckpoint
   704→	}
   705→
   706→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   707→	if err != nil {
   708→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   709→	}
   710→
   711→	// Get the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   712→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   713→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   714→	if err != nil {
   715→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("metadata branch %s not found: %w", paths.MetadataBranchName, err)
   716→	}
   717→
   718→	metadataCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   719→	if err != nil {
   720→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata branch commit: %w", err)
   721→	}
   722→
   723→	tree, err := metadataCommit.Tree()
   724→	if err != nil {
   725→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata tree: %w", err)
   726→	}
   727→
   728→	// Find checkpoint using the metadata path from rewind point
   729→	// MetadataDir for auto-commit task checkpoints is: cond-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-XXXXXXXX/tasks/<tool-use-id>
   730→	checkpointPath := point.MetadataDir + "/checkpoint.json"
   731→	file, err := tree.File(checkpointPath)
   732→	if err != nil {
   733→		// Try finding via commit SHA lookup
   734→		taskCheckpointPath, findErr := s.findTaskCheckpointPath(repo, point.ID, point.ToolUseID)
   735→		if findErr != nil {
   736→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find checkpoint at %s: %w", checkpointPath, err)
   737→		}
   738→		file, err = tree.File(taskCheckpointPath)
   739→		if err != nil {
   740→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find checkpoint at %s: %w", taskCheckpointPath, err)
   741→		}
   742→	}
   743→
   744→	content, err := file.Contents()
   745→	if err != nil {
   746→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   747→	}
   748→
   749→	var checkpoint TaskCheckpoint
   750→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &checkpoint); err != nil {
   751→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse checkpoint: %w", err)
   752→	}
   753→
   754→	return &checkpoint, nil
   755→}
   756→
   757→// GetTaskCheckpointTranscript returns the session transcript for a task checkpoint.
   758→// For auto-commit strategy, transcripts are stored on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch in checkpoint directories.
   759→// Returns ErrNotTaskCheckpoint if the point is not a task checkpoint.
   760→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetTaskCheckpointTranscript(point RewindPoint) ([]byte, error) {
   761→	if !point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
   762→		return nil, ErrNotTaskCheckpoint
   763→	}
   764→
   765→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   766→	if err != nil {
   767→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   768→	}
   769→
   770→	// Get the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   771→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   772→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   773→	if err != nil {
   774→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("metadata branch %s not found: %w", paths.MetadataBranchName, err)
   775→	}
   776→
   777→	metadataCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   778→	if err != nil {
   779→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata branch commit: %w", err)
   780→	}
   781→
   782→	tree, err := metadataCommit.Tree()
   783→	if err != nil {
   784→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata tree: %w", err)
   785→	}
   786→
   787→	// MetadataDir for auto-commit task checkpoints is: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/tasks/<tool-use-id>
   788→	// Extract the checkpoint path by removing "/tasks/<tool-use-id>"
   789→	metadataDir := point.MetadataDir
   790→	if idx := strings.Index(metadataDir, "/tasks/"); idx > 0 {
   791→		checkpointPath := metadataDir[:idx]
   792→
   793→		// Use the first session's transcript path from sessions array
   794→		transcriptPath := ""
   795→		summaryFile, summaryErr := tree.File(checkpointPath + "/" + paths.MetadataFileName)
   796→		if summaryErr == nil {
   797→			summaryContent, contentErr := summaryFile.Contents()
   798→			if contentErr == nil {
   799→				var summary checkpoint.CheckpointSummary
   800→				if json.Unmarshal([]byte(summaryContent), &summary) == nil && len(summary.Sessions) > 0 {
   801→					// Use first session's transcript path (task checkpoints have only one session)
   802→					// SessionFilePaths now contains absolute paths with leading "/"
   803→					// Strip the leading "/" for tree.File() which expects paths without leading slash
   804→					if summary.Sessions[0].Transcript != "" {
   805→						transcriptPath = strings.TrimPrefix(summary.Sessions[0].Transcript, "/")
   806→					}
   807→				}
   808→			}
   809→		}
   810→
   811→		// Fall back to old format if sessions map not available
   812→		if transcriptPath == "" {
   813→			transcriptPath = checkpointPath + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName
   814→		}
   815→
   816→		file, err := tree.File(transcriptPath)
   817→		if err != nil {
   818→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find transcript at %s: %w", transcriptPath, err)
   819→		}
   820→		content, err := file.Contents()
   821→		if err != nil {
   822→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   823→		}
   824→		return []byte(content), nil
   825→	}
   826→
   827→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid metadata path format: %s", metadataDir)
   828→}
   829→
   830→// findTaskCheckpointPath finds the full path to a task checkpoint on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   831→// Searches checkpoint directories for the task checkpoint matching the commit SHA and tool use ID.
   832→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) findTaskCheckpointPath(repo *git.Repository, commitSHA, toolUseID string) (string, error) {
   833→	// Use findTaskMetadataPathForCommit which searches commit history
   834→	taskPath, err := s.findTaskMetadataPathForCommit(repo, commitSHA, toolUseID)
   835→	if err != nil {
   836→		return "", err
   837→	}
   838→	if taskPath == "" {
   839→		return "", errors.New("task checkpoint not found")
   840→	}
   841→	// taskPath is like: cond-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-XXXXXXXX/tasks/<tool-use-id>/checkpoints/001-<tool-use-id>.json
   842→	// We need: cond-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-XXXXXXXX/tasks/<tool-use-id>/checkpoint.json
   843→	if idx := strings.Index(taskPath, "/checkpoints/"); idx > 0 {
   844→		return taskPath[:idx] + "/checkpoint.json", nil
   845→	}
   846→	return taskPath + "/checkpoint.json", nil
   847→}
   848→
   849→// GetMetadataRef returns a reference to the metadata for the given checkpoint.
   850→// For auto-commit strategy, returns the checkpoint path on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   851→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetMetadataRef(checkpoint Checkpoint) string {
   852→	if checkpoint.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   853→		return ""
   854→	}
   855→	return paths.MetadataBranchName + ":" + checkpoint.CheckpointID.Path()
   856→}
   857→
   858→// GetSessionMetadataRef returns a reference to the most recent metadata for a session.
   859→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetSessionMetadataRef(sessionID string) string {
   860→	session, err := GetSession(sessionID)
   861→	if err != nil || len(session.Checkpoints) == 0 {
   862→		return ""
   863→	}
   864→	// Checkpoints are ordered with most recent first
   865→	return s.GetMetadataRef(session.Checkpoints[0])
   866→}
   867→
   868→// GetSessionContext returns the context.md content for a session.
   869→// For auto-commit strategy, reads from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch using the checkpoint store.
   870→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetSessionContext(sessionID string) string {
   871→	session, err := GetSession(sessionID)
   872→	if err != nil || len(session.Checkpoints) == 0 {
   873→		return ""
   874→	}
   875→
   876→	// Get the most recent checkpoint
   877→	cp := session.Checkpoints[0]
   878→	if cp.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   879→		return ""
   880→	}
   881→
   882→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   883→	if err != nil {
   884→		return ""
   885→	}
   886→
   887→	content, err := store.ReadSessionContentByID(context.Background(), cp.CheckpointID, sessionID)
   888→	if err != nil || content == nil {
   889→		return ""
   890→	}
   891→
   892→	return content.Context
   893→}
   894→
   895→// GetCheckpointLog returns the session transcript for a specific checkpoint.
   896→// For auto-commit strategy, looks up checkpoint by ID on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch using the checkpoint store.
   897→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetCheckpointLog(cp Checkpoint) ([]byte, error) {
   898→	if cp.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   899→		return nil, ErrNoMetadata
   900→	}
   901→
   902→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   903→	if err != nil {
   904→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   905→	}
   906→
   907→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), cp.CheckpointID)
   908→	if err != nil {
   909→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   910→	}
   911→	if content == nil {
   912→		return nil, ErrNoMetadata
   913→	}
   914→
   915→	return content.Transcript, nil
   916→}
   917→
   918→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session.
   919→// This is called during UserPromptSubmit hook to set up tracking for the session.
   920→// For auto-commit strategy, this creates a SessionState file in .git/entire-sessions/
   921→// to track CheckpointTranscriptStart (transcript offset) across checkpoints.
   922→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   923→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   924→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
   925→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) error {
   926→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   927→	if err != nil {
   928→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   929→	}
   930→
   931→	// Get current HEAD commit to track as base
   932→	head, err := repo.Head()
   933→	if err != nil {
   934→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   935→	}
   936→
   937→	baseCommit := head.Hash().String()
   938→
   939→	// Check if session state already exists (e.g., session resuming)
   940→	existing, err := LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   941→	if err != nil {
   942→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check existing session state: %w", err)
   943→	}
   944→	if existing != nil {
   945→		// Session already initialized — update last interaction time on every prompt submit
   946→		now := time.Now()
   947→		existing.LastInteractionTime = &now
   948→
   949→		// Generate a new TurnID for each turn (correlates carry-forward checkpoints)
   950→		turnID, err := id.Generate()
   951→		if err != nil {
   952→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate turn ID: %w", err)
   953→		}
   954→		existing.TurnID = turnID.String()
   955→		existing.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
   956→
   957→		// Backfill FirstPrompt if empty (for sessions
   958→		// created before the first_prompt field was added, or resumed sessions)
   959→		if existing.FirstPrompt == "" && userPrompt != "" {
   960→			existing.FirstPrompt = truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt)
   961→		}
   962→
   963→		if err := SaveSessionState(existing); err != nil {
   964→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to update session state: %w", err)
   965→		}
   966→		return nil
   967→	}
   968→
   969→	// Generate TurnID for the first turn
   970→	turnID, err := id.Generate()
   971→	if err != nil {
   972→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate turn ID: %w", err)
   973→	}
   974→
   975→	// Create new session state
   976→	now := time.Now()
   977→	state := &SessionState{
   978→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   979→		CLIVersion:          buildinfo.Version,
   980→		BaseCommit:          baseCommit,
   981→		StartedAt:           now,
   982→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   983→		TurnID:              turnID.String(),
   984→		StepCount:           0,
   985→		// CheckpointTranscriptStart defaults to 0 (start from beginning of transcript)
   986→		FilesTouched:   []string{},
   987→		AgentType:      agentType,
   988→		TranscriptPath: transcriptPath,
   989→		FirstPrompt:    truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt),
   990→	}
   991→
   992→	if err := SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   993→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   994→	}
   995→
   996→	return nil
   997→}
   998→
   999→// ListOrphanedItems returns orphaned items created by the auto-commit strategy.
  1000→// For auto-commit, checkpoints are orphaned when no commit has an Entire-Checkpoint
  1001→// trailer referencing them (e.g., after rebasing or squashing).
  1002→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) ListOrphanedItems() ([]CleanupItem, error) {
  1003→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
  1004→	if err != nil {
  1005→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
  1006→	}
  1007→
  1008→	// Get checkpoint store (lazily initialized)
  1009→	cpStore, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
  1010→	if err != nil {
  1011→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
  1012→	}
  1013→
  1014→	// Get all checkpoints from entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
  1015→	checkpoints, err := cpStore.ListCommitted(context.Background())
  1016→	if err != nil {
  1017→		return []CleanupItem{}, nil //nolint:nilerr // No checkpoints is not an error for cleanup
  1018→	}
  1019→
  1020→	if len(checkpoints) == 0 {
  1021→		return []CleanupItem{}, nil
  1022→	}
  1023→
  1024→	// Filter to only auto-commit checkpoints (identified by strategy in metadata)
  1025→	autoCommitCheckpoints := make(map[string]bool)
  1026→	for _, cp := range checkpoints {
  1027→		summary, readErr := cpStore.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), cp.CheckpointID)
  1028→		if readErr != nil || summary == nil {
  1029→			continue
  1030→		}
  1031→		// Only consider checkpoints created by this strategy
  1032→		if summary.Strategy == StrategyNameAutoCommit {
  1033→			autoCommitCheckpoints[cp.CheckpointID.String()] = true
  1034→		}
  1035→	}
  1036→
  1037→	if len(autoCommitCheckpoints) == 0 {
  1038→		return []CleanupItem{}, nil
  1039→	}
  1040→
  1041→	// Find checkpoint IDs referenced in commits
  1042→	referencedCheckpoints := s.findReferencedCheckpoints(repo)
  1043→
  1044→	// Find orphaned checkpoints
  1045→	var items []CleanupItem
  1046→	for checkpointID := range autoCommitCheckpoints {
  1047→		if !referencedCheckpoints[checkpointID] {
  1048→			items = append(items, CleanupItem{
  1049→				Type:   CleanupTypeCheckpoint,
  1050→				ID:     checkpointID,
  1051→				Reason: "no commit references this checkpoint",
  1052→			})
  1053→		}
  1054→	}
  1055→
  1056→	return items, nil
  1057→}
  1058→
  1059→// findReferencedCheckpoints scans commits for Entire-Checkpoint trailers.
  1060→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) findReferencedCheckpoints(repo *git.Repository) map[string]bool {
  1061→	referenced := make(map[string]bool)
  1062→
  1063→	refs, err := repo.References()
  1064→	if err != nil {
  1065→		return referenced
  1066→	}
  1067→
  1068→	visited := make(map[plumbing.Hash]bool)
  1069→
  1070→	_ = refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best effort
  1071→		if !ref.Name().IsBranch() {
  1072→			return nil
  1073→		}
  1074→		// Skip entire/* branches
  1075→		branchName := strings.TrimPrefix(ref.Name().String(), "refs/heads/")
  1076→		if strings.HasPrefix(branchName, "entire/") {
  1077→			return nil
  1078→		}
  1079→
  1080→		iter, iterErr := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: ref.Hash()})
  1081→		if iterErr != nil {
  1082→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Best effort
  1083→		}
  1084→
  1085→		count := 0
  1086→		_ = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best effort
  1087→			count++
  1088→			if count > 1000 {
  1089→				return errors.New("limit reached")
  1090→			}
  1091→			if visited[c.Hash] {
  1092→				return nil
  1093→			}
  1094→			visited[c.Hash] = true
  1095→
  1096→			if cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message); found {
  1097→				referenced[cpID.String()] = true
  1098→			}
  1099→			return nil
  1100→		})
  1101→		return nil
  1102→	})
  1103→
  1104→	return referenced
  1105→}
  1106→
  1107→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Standard pattern for strategy registration
  1108→func init() {
  1109→	// Register auto-commit as the primary strategy name
  1110→	Register(StrategyNameAutoCommit, NewAutoCommitStrategy)
  1111→}
  1112→
```

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"slices"
     6→	"sync"
     7→)
     8→
     9→var (
    10→	registryMu sync.RWMutex
    11→	registry   = make(map[AgentName]Factory)
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Factory creates a new agent instance
    15→type Factory func() Agent
    16→
    17→// Register adds an agent factory to the registry.
    18→// Called from init() in each agent implementation.
    19→func Register(name AgentName, factory Factory) {
    20→	registryMu.Lock()
    21→	defer registryMu.Unlock()
    22→	registry[name] = factory
    23→}
    24→
    25→// Get retrieves an agent by name.
    26→//
    27→
    28→func Get(name AgentName) (Agent, error) {
    29→	registryMu.RLock()
    30→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    31→
    32→	factory, ok := registry[name]
    33→	if !ok {
    34→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s (available: %v)", name, List())
    35→	}
    36→	return factory(), nil
    37→}
    38→
    39→// List returns all registered agent names in sorted order.
    40→func List() []AgentName {
    41→	registryMu.RLock()
    42→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    43→
    44→	names := make([]AgentName, 0, len(registry))
    45→	for name := range registry {
    46→		names = append(names, name)
    47→	}
    48→	slices.Sort(names)
    49→	return names
    50→}
    51→
    52→// DetectAll returns all agents whose DetectPresence reports true.
    53→// Agents are checked in sorted name order (via List()) for deterministic results.
    54→// Returns an empty slice when no agent is detected.
    55→func DetectAll() []Agent {
    56→	names := List() // sorted, lock-safe
    57→
    58→	var detected []Agent
    59→	for _, name := range names {
    60→		ag, err := Get(name)
    61→		if err != nil {
    62→			continue
    63→		}
    64→		if present, err := ag.DetectPresence(); err == nil && present {
    65→			detected = append(detected, ag)
    66→		}
    67→	}
    68→	return detected
    69→}
    70→
    71→// Detect attempts to auto-detect which agent is being used.
    72→// Iterates registered agents in sorted name order for deterministic results.
    73→// Returns the first agent whose DetectPresence reports true.
    74→func Detect() (Agent, error) {
    75→	detected := DetectAll()
    76→	if len(detected) == 0 {
    77→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no agent detected (available: %v)", List())
    78→	}
    79→	return detected[0], nil
    80→}
    81→
    82→// AgentName is the registry key type for agents (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini").
    83→//
    84→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentType when both are used
    85→type AgentName string
    86→
    87→// AgentType is the display name type stored in metadata/trailers (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI").
    88→//
    89→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentName when both are used
    90→type AgentType string
    91→
    92→// Agent name constants (registry keys)
    93→const (
    94→	AgentNameClaudeCode AgentName = "claude-code"
    95→	AgentNameGemini     AgentName = "gemini"
    96→	AgentNameOpenCode   AgentName = "opencode"
    97→)
    98→
    99→// Agent type constants (type identifiers stored in metadata/trailers)
   100→const (
   101→	AgentTypeClaudeCode AgentType = "Claude Code"
   102→	AgentTypeGemini     AgentType = "Gemini CLI"
   103→	AgentTypeOpenCode   AgentType = "OpenCode"
   104→	AgentTypeUnknown    AgentType = "Agent" // Fallback for backwards compatibility
   105→)
   106→
   107→// DefaultAgentName is the registry key for the default agent.
   108→const DefaultAgentName AgentName = AgentNameClaudeCode
   109→
   110→// GetByAgentType retrieves an agent by its type identifier.
   111→//
   112→// Note: This uses a linear search that instantiates agents until a match is found.
   113→// This is acceptable because:
   114→//   - Agent count is small (~2-20 agents)
   115→//   - Agent factories are lightweight (empty struct allocation)
   116→//   - Called infrequently (commit hooks, rewind, debug commands - not hot paths)
   117→//   - Cost is ~400ns worst case vs milliseconds for I/O operations
   118→//
   119→// Only optimize if agent count exceeds 100 or profiling shows this as a bottleneck.
   120→func GetByAgentType(agentType AgentType) (Agent, error) {
   121→	registryMu.RLock()
   122→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
   123→
   124→	for _, factory := range registry {
   125→		ag := factory()
   126→		if ag.Type() == agentType {
   127→			return ag, nil
   128→		}
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent type: %s", agentType)
   132→}
   133→
   134→// AllProtectedDirs returns the union of ProtectedDirs from all registered agents.
   135→func AllProtectedDirs() []string {
   136→	// Copy factories under the lock, then release before calling external code.
   137→	registryMu.RLock()
   138→	factories := make([]Factory, 0, len(registry))
   139→	for _, f := range registry {
   140→		factories = append(factories, f)
   141→	}
   142→	registryMu.RUnlock()
   143→
   144→	seen := make(map[string]struct{})
   145→	var dirs []string
   146→	for _, factory := range factories {
   147→		for _, d := range factory().ProtectedDirs() {
   148→			if _, ok := seen[d]; !ok {
   149→				seen[d] = struct{}{}
   150→				dirs = append(dirs, d)
   151→			}
   152→		}
   153→	}
   154→	slices.Sort(dirs)
   155→	return dirs
   156→}
   157→
   158→// Default returns the default agent.
   159→// Returns nil if the default agent is not registered.
   160→//
   161→//nolint:errcheck // Factory pattern returns interface; error is acceptable to ignore for default
   162→func Default() Agent {
   163→	a, _ := Get(DefaultAgentName)
   164→	return a
   165→}
   166→

     1→// Package trail provides types and helpers for managing trail metadata.
     2→// Trails are branch-centric work tracking abstractions stored on the
     3→// entire/trails orphan branch. They answer "why/what" (human intent)
     4→// while checkpoints answer "how/when" (machine snapshots).
     5→package trail
     6→
     7→import (
     8→	"crypto/rand"
     9→	"encoding/hex"
    10→	"fmt"
    11→	"regexp"
    12→	"strings"
    13→	"time"
    14→)
    15→
    16→const idLength = 6 // 6 bytes = 12 hex chars
    17→
    18→// ID is a 12-character hex identifier for trails.
    19→type ID string
    20→
    21→// EmptyID represents an unset or invalid trail ID.
    22→const EmptyID ID = ""
    23→
    24→// idRegex validates the format: exactly 12 lowercase hex characters.
    25→var idRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{12}$`)
    26→
    27→// GenerateID creates a new random 12-character hex trail ID.
    28→func GenerateID() (ID, error) {
    29→	bytes := make([]byte, idLength)
    30→	if _, err := rand.Read(bytes); err != nil {
    31→		return EmptyID, fmt.Errorf("failed to generate random trail ID: %w", err)
    32→	}
    33→	return ID(hex.EncodeToString(bytes)), nil
    34→}
    35→
    36→// ValidateID checks if a string is a valid trail ID format.
    37→func ValidateID(s string) error {
    38→	if !idRegex.MatchString(s) {
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid trail ID %q: must be 12 lowercase hex characters", s)
    40→	}
    41→	return nil
    42→}
    43→
    44→// String returns the trail ID as a string.
    45→func (id ID) String() string {
    46→	return string(id)
    47→}
    48→
    49→// IsEmpty returns true if the trail ID is empty or unset.
    50→func (id ID) IsEmpty() bool {
    51→	return id == EmptyID
    52→}
    53→
    54→// Path returns the sharded storage path for this trail ID.
    55→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
    56→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
    57→func (id ID) Path() string {
    58→	if len(id) < 3 {
    59→		return string(id)
    60→	}
    61→	return string(id[:2]) + "/" + string(id[2:])
    62→}
    63→
    64→// Status represents the lifecycle status of a trail.
    65→type Status string
    66→
    67→const (
    68→	StatusDraft      Status = "draft"
    69→	StatusOpen       Status = "open"
    70→	StatusInProgress Status = "in_progress"
    71→	StatusInReview   Status = "in_review"
    72→	StatusDone       Status = "done"
    73→	StatusClosed     Status = "closed"
    74→)
    75→
    76→// ValidStatuses returns all valid trail statuses in lifecycle order.
    77→func ValidStatuses() []Status {
    78→	return []Status{
    79→		StatusDraft,
    80→		StatusOpen,
    81→		StatusInProgress,
    82→		StatusInReview,
    83→		StatusDone,
    84→		StatusClosed,
    85→	}
    86→}
    87→
    88→// IsValid returns true if the status is a recognized trail status.
    89→func (s Status) IsValid() bool {
    90→	for _, vs := range ValidStatuses() {
    91→		if s == vs {
    92→			return true
    93→		}
    94→	}
    95→	return false
    96→}
    97→
    98→// Metadata represents the metadata for a trail, matching the web PR format.
    99→type Metadata struct {
   100→	TrailID     ID         `json:"trail_id"`
   101→	Branch      string     `json:"branch"`
   102→	Base        string     `json:"base"`
   103→	Title       string     `json:"title"`
   104→	Description string     `json:"description"`
   105→	Status      Status     `json:"status"`
   106→	Author      string     `json:"author"`
   107→	Assignees   []string   `json:"assignees"`
   108→	Labels      []string   `json:"labels"`
   109→	CreatedAt   time.Time  `json:"created_at"`
   110→	UpdatedAt   time.Time  `json:"updated_at"`
   111→	MergedAt    *time.Time `json:"merged_at,omitempty"`
   112→}
   113→
   114→// Discussion holds the discussion/comments for a trail.
   115→type Discussion struct {
   116→	Comments []Comment `json:"comments"`
   117→}
   118→
   119→// Comment represents a single comment on a trail.
   120→type Comment struct {
   121→	ID        string         `json:"id"`
   122→	Author    string         `json:"author"`
   123→	Body      string         `json:"body"`
   124→	CreatedAt time.Time      `json:"created_at"`
   125→	Replies   []CommentReply `json:"replies,omitempty"`
   126→}
   127→
   128→// CommentReply represents a reply to a comment.
   129→type CommentReply struct {
   130→	ID        string    `json:"id"`
   131→	Author    string    `json:"author"`
   132→	Body      string    `json:"body"`
   133→	CreatedAt time.Time `json:"created_at"`
   134→}
   135→
   136→// commonBranchPrefixes are stripped from branch names when humanizing.
   137→var commonBranchPrefixes = []string{
   138→	"feature/",
   139→	"fix/",
   140→	"bugfix/",
   141→	"chore/",
   142→	"hotfix/",
   143→	"release/",
   144→}
   145→
   146→// CheckpointRef links a checkpoint to a trail.
   147→type CheckpointRef struct {
   148→	CheckpointID string    `json:"checkpoint_id"`
   149→	CommitSHA    string    `json:"commit_sha"`
   150→	CreatedAt    time.Time `json:"created_at"`
   151→	Summary      string    `json:"summary,omitempty"`
   152→}
   153→
   154→// Checkpoints holds the list of checkpoint references for a trail.
   155→type Checkpoints struct {
   156→	Checkpoints []CheckpointRef `json:"checkpoints"`
   157→}
   158→
   159→// HumanizeBranchName converts a branch name into a human-readable title.
   160→// It strips common prefixes (feature/, fix/, etc.), replaces dashes/underscores
   161→// with spaces, and capitalizes the first word.
   162→func HumanizeBranchName(branch string) string {
   163→	name := branch
   164→	for _, prefix := range commonBranchPrefixes {
   165→		if strings.HasPrefix(name, prefix) {
   166→			name = strings.TrimPrefix(name, prefix)
   167→			break
   168→		}
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// Replace - and _ with spaces
   172→	name = strings.NewReplacer("-", " ", "_", " ").Replace(name)
   173→
   174→	// Trim spaces and capitalize first letter
   175→	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
   176→	if name == "" {
   177→		return branch
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	// Capitalize first character
   181→	return strings.ToUpper(name[:1]) + name[1:]
   182→}
   183→
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/generate.go",
  "content": "package claudecode\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"strings\"\n)\n\n// GenerateText sends a prompt to the Claude CLI and returns the raw text response.\n// Implements the agent.TextGenerator interface.\n// The model parameter hints which model to use (e.g., \"haiku\", \"sonnet\").\n// If empty, defaults to \"haiku\" for fast, cheap generation.\nfunc (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GenerateText(ctx context.Context, prompt string, model string) (string, error) {\n\tclaudePath := \"claude\"\n\tif model == \"\" {\n\t\tmodel = \"haiku\"\n\t}\n\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, claudePath,\n\t\t\"--print\", \"--output-format\", \"json\",\n\t\t\"--model\", model, \"--setting-sources\", \"\")\n\n\t// Isolate from the user's git repo to prevent recursive hook triggers\n\t// and index pollution (same approach as summarize/claude.go).\n\tcmd.Dir = os.TempDir()\n\tcmd.Env = stripGitEnv(os.Environ())\n\tcmd.Stdin = strings.NewReader(prompt)\n\n\tvar stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\tif err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\tvar execErr *exec.Error\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &execErr) {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"claude CLI not found: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar exitErr *exec.ExitError\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &exitErr) {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"claude CLI failed (exit %d): %s\", exitErr.ExitCode(), stderr.String())\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to run claude CLI: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Parse the {\"result\": \"...\"} envelope\n\tvar response struct {\n\t\tResult string `json:\"result\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(stdout.Bytes(), &response); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse claude CLI response: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn response.Result, nil\n}\n\n// stripGitEnv returns a copy of env with all GIT_* variables removed.\n// This prevents a subprocess from discovering or modifying the parent's git repo.\n// Duplicated from summarize/claude.go — simple filter not worth extracting to shared package.\nfunc stripGitEnv(env []string) []string {\n\tfiltered := make([]string, 0, len(env))\n\tfor _, e := range env {\n\t\tif !strings.HasPrefix(e, \"GIT_\") {\n\t\t\tfiltered = append(filtered, e)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn filtered\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/summarize/trail_title.go",
  "content": "package summarize\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n)\n\n// trailTitlePromptTemplate is the prompt used to generate trail titles and descriptions.\n//\n// Security note: The transcript is wrapped in <transcript> tags to provide clear boundary\n// markers. This helps contain any potentially malicious content within the transcript.\nconst trailTitlePromptTemplate = `Analyze this development session transcript and generate a title and description.\n\n<transcript>\n%s\n</transcript>\n\nReturn a JSON object:\n{\n  \"title\": \"Short imperative title (max 80 chars)\",\n  \"description\": \"1-3 sentence description of what was accomplished and why\"\n}\n\nGuidelines:\n- Title: imperative mood, captures core intent (e.g. \"Add user authentication flow\")\n- Description: explain the \"what\" and \"why\", not the \"how\"\n- Return ONLY the JSON object`\n\n// trailTitleModel is the model hint for trail title generation.\n// Haiku is fast (~1-2s) and cheap — trail titles are simple tasks.\nconst trailTitleModel = \"haiku\"\n\n// TrailTitleResult contains the LLM-generated title and description for a trail.\ntype TrailTitleResult struct {\n\tTitle       string `json:\"title\"`\n\tDescription string `json:\"description\"`\n}\n\n// GenerateTrailTitle generates a title and description for a trail using the agent's\n// text generation capability. Returns nil if the agent doesn't support text generation.\nfunc GenerateTrailTitle(ctx context.Context, transcriptBytes []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType) (*TrailTitleResult, error) {\n\t// Get the active agent and check if it implements TextGenerator\n\tag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"agent not found: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tgen, ok := ag.(agent.TextGenerator)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"agent %s does not support text generation\", agentType)\n\t}\n\n\t// Build condensed transcript (reuse existing infrastructure)\n\tcondensed, err := BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes(transcriptBytes, agentType)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(condensed) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"transcript has no content\")\n\t}\n\n\tinput := Input{Transcript: condensed, FilesTouched: filesTouched}\n\ttranscriptText := FormatCondensedTranscript(input)\n\n\t// Build prompt and call agent's TextGenerator\n\tprompt := fmt.Sprintf(trailTitlePromptTemplate, transcriptText)\n\trawResult, err := gen.GenerateText(ctx, prompt, trailTitleModel)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"text generation failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Parse JSON response (handle markdown code blocks)\n\tcleaned := extractJSONFromMarkdown(rawResult)\n\tvar result TrailTitleResult\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(cleaned), &result); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse trail title JSON: %w (response: %s)\", err, cleaned)\n\t}\n\n\treturn &result, nil\n}\n"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/dip/Repositories/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go",
  "old_string": "// appendCheckpointToTrail links a checkpoint to the trail for the current branch.",
  "new_string": "// generateTrailTitleFromTranscript uses the agent's text generation capability\n// to generate a proper title and description for the trail. Best-effort: silently\n// returns on any error. Only generates once (skips if description already set).\nfunc generateTrailTitleFromTranscript(repo *git.Repository, branchName string, transcriptBytes []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType) {\n\tif !settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tstore := trail.NewStore(repo)\n\texisting, err := store.FindByBranch(branchName)\n\tif err != nil || existing == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t// Only generate once: skip if description already set\n\tif existing.Description != \"\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"trail-title\")\n\tresult, err := summarize.GenerateTrailTitle(logCtx, transcriptBytes, filesTouched, agentType)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"trail title generation skipped\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", err.Error()))\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t//nolint:errcheck // best-effort: trail title generation is non-critical\n\tstore.Update(existing.TrailID, func(m *trail.Metadata) {\n\t\tif result.Title != \"\" {\n\t\t\tm.Title = result.Title\n\t\t}\n\t\tif result.Description != \"\" {\n\t\t\tm.Description = result.Description\n\t\t}\n\t})\n}\n\n// appendCheckpointToTrail links a checkpoint to the trail for the current branch."
}
```

     1→package summarize
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→
     8→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
     9→)
    10→
    11→// trailTitlePromptTemplate is the prompt used to generate trail titles and descriptions.
    12→//
    13→// Security note: The transcript is wrapped in <transcript> tags to provide clear boundary
    14→// markers. This helps contain any potentially malicious content within the transcript.
    15→const trailTitlePromptTemplate = `Analyze this development session transcript and generate a title and description.
    16→
    17→<transcript>
    18→%s
    19→</transcript>
    20→
    21→Return a JSON object:
    22→{
    23→  "title": "Short imperative title (max 80 chars)",
    24→  "description": "1-3 sentence description of what was accomplished and why"
    25→}
    26→
    27→Guidelines:
    28→- Title: imperative mood, captures core intent (e.g. "Add user authentication flow")
    29→- Description: explain the "what" and "why", not the "how"
    30→- Return ONLY the JSON object`
    31→
    32→// trailTitleModel is the model hint for trail title generation.
    33→// Haiku is fast (~1-2s) and cheap — trail titles are simple tasks.
    34→const trailTitleModel = "haiku"
    35→
    36→// TrailTitleResult contains the LLM-generated title and description for a trail.
    37→type TrailTitleResult struct {
    38→	Title       string `json:"title"`
    39→	Description string `json:"description"`
    40→}
    41→
    42→// GenerateTrailTitle generates a title and description for a trail using the agent's
    43→// text generation capability. Returns nil if the agent doesn't support text generation.
    44→func GenerateTrailTitle(ctx context.Context, transcriptBytes []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType) (*TrailTitleResult, error) {
    45→	// Get the active agent and check if it implements TextGenerator
    46→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent not found: %w", err)
    49→	}
    50→	gen, ok := ag.(agent.TextGenerator)
    51→	if !ok {
    52→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("agent %s does not support text generation", agentType)
    53→	}
    54→
    55→	// Build condensed transcript (reuse existing infrastructure)
    56→	condensed, err := BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes(transcriptBytes, agentType)
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    59→	}
    60→	if len(condensed) == 0 {
    61→		return nil, errors.New("transcript has no content")
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	input := Input{Transcript: condensed, FilesTouched: filesTouched}
    65→	transcriptText := FormatCondensedTranscript(input)
    66→
    67→	// Build prompt and call agent's TextGenerator
    68→	prompt := fmt.Sprintf(trailTitlePromptTemplate, transcriptText)
    69→	rawResult, err := gen.GenerateText(ctx, prompt, trailTitleModel)
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("text generation failed: %w", err)
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// Parse JSON response (handle markdown code blocks)

    78→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse trail title JSON: %w (response: %s)", err, cleaned)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	return &result, nil
    82→}

All done. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

## Changes

### C1. `TextGenerator` interface (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go`)
- Added `TextGenerator` optional interface with `GenerateText(ctx, prompt, model) (string, error)` method
- Follows existing pattern alongside `TranscriptAnalyzer`, `HookSupport`, etc.

### C2. Claude Code implementation (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/generate.go`) — **new file**
- Implements `TextGenerator` for `ClaudeCodeAgent`
- Uses `claude --print --output-format json --model <model> --setting-sources ""`
- Isolates from git repo (strips `GIT_*` env vars, runs in temp dir)
- Parses `{"result": "..."}` envelope from Claude CLI

### C3. Trail title generation (`cmd/entire/cli/summarize/trail_title.go`) — **new file**
- `GenerateTrailTitle()` function that uses `TextGenerator` to generate title + description
- Reuses existing transcript condensation infrastructure
- Prompt template asks for imperative title (max 80 chars) and 1-3 sentence description
- Uses haiku model for speed/cost

### C4. `Transcript` field on `CondenseResult` (`manual_commit_types.go` + `manual_commit_condensation.go`)
- Added `Transcript []byte` field to `CondenseResult`
- Populated from `sessionData.Transcript` in `CondenseSession()`

### C5. Wiring (`manual_commit_hooks.go` + `auto_commit.go`)
- Added `generateTrailTitleFromTranscript()` helper — gated by `IsSummarizeEnabled()`, only generates once (skips if `Description != ""`)
- **Manual-commit**: Called after `appendCheckpointToTrail()` in `condenseAndUpdateState()`
- **Auto-commit**: Called after `appendCheckpointToTrail()` in `SaveStep()`, reads transcript from `ctx.TranscriptPath`
